Day 181 | Ember 2, Y.H. 813 (Secondday) | the Vail wheel-shed and its finger's-width-open back room (not entered), the cold damper, the hearth and loaf shelf, the kiln mouth, the drying racks, the yard well, the flagstones and the kindling, the clay road at the going of the light, the bed under the Vail roof, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | Before first grey three or four men went up the clay road with their collars high and went low through the finger's-width door of the back room and were given no name, Sella Vail carrying past at the half-pace, turning no head, giving the house no name for them and not saying the room was cold, while the two tellings of a counting room shut in the war stood side by side and no third came (T035/T031, T065/T095); Jorra Vail came through with the day's grey slate dust ground small as meal into both forearms past the bent little finger and under the nails, white in the creases where a fist had opened and not the bone-coloured slip of the wheel, kept dusty forearms and clean ones on one tray, asked neither which slate had been broken nor whether it was a birth-slate, spoke to the wheel before anyone (*It doesn't care whose hands. It cares if they're steady*), told a pot *You'll do*, knew the note it would give three turns before it left the wheel and withheld the knowing from a High Kiln kiln-master come up on the wrong wind with the sweetish edge of lead glaze at one mouth-corner who said a pot singing before its time generally sings itself out early, the knowing going to the near-full tray beside the hearings and never among them, advanced and not paid, and no pot lost in the yard (T061/T082); at the hearth, when Sella's voice went low and unfinished (*The fire's low. It wants---*), his bent finger went to the cold iron of the damper ahead of his hand and **held** while she finished *feeding* and gave the end to the pot, then moved the tongue a hair's breadth and left the finger standing in the iron, the fraction remembered in the holding and not in a word, no payer named and not paired with the shelf beat or the flagstone half-check (T062), while the boy read one piece of iron with two bites, the holding and the taking, with no Grathic word for both mercies, joined to no kiln and entered in no slate; Tarn took the broom only after it had been instructed, six counted where the house could check and the seventh at the lip where it could not, the Seed 16 breath unspent, his stroke going over the bright patch without the half-check and the three held changes still on three places, while at the racks the yard went around the unswept lines, the birth-slates, the pan's gone ring and the field's empty place with a look and a wide step and no number (T036/T076, T009/T013/T092, T098 carried); a neighbour come up from the tubs with a wet pot asked what the newly opened firing wanted set down under this roof and Sella's eye went once to the cold clay jar without a touch before she sent the question to the pot and the weather with *Ask the pot what a wet thing costs set down there. The house has said it*, the neighbour carrying it away level, nothing slopped, nothing priced, nothing refused, no entry left (T077); from the basket's low angle the loaf shelf's underside became a roof and the charcoal waited under its front edge for a hand that was not hers and a day that had not come, her finger staying from beneath the board, the boy reading nothing, counting nothing, copying nothing and showing nothing to anybody (T100), and in the small hours the file came back exact, complete and worth nothing with one hundred and eighty-one unspent in the dark of the wool, the fire's fifth still missing, the ninth stopping still nine, the Seed 16 breath still held, the mark touched once before the kiln and at no other hour.
Day 182 | Ember 3, Y.H. 813 (Thirdday) | the Clay Yard and the kiln mouth, the drying racks and the yard well, the wheel-shed edge, the flagstones, the kindling, the loaf shelf, the Vail kiln-house hearth and the bed under the roof, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | The third day of the kiln month came in without anybody marking where the second had ended and the second working day was ordinary tending, the kiln drawing breath behind the wall in fours with a gap and the carried fire under the floor answering four low pulls with the silence where the fifth should have been, its payer without a name for a hundred and eighty-two days, the river keeping its note of water over water through the finger's-width door as a fifth low thing never counted among the hearings and the boards keeping four nails, four and a thumb-width knot --- three counts in three places that did not become one (T088, T061/T082); the month's danger had an order and no name and could not be carried into a chart, hot clay under tallow, fine grey slate dust ground small as meal sitting white in the crease of a knuckle when a fist opens, the sweetish edge of lead glaze riding up from High Kiln on a wrong wind, and a pot set down wrong on a rack's edge giving its high thin note before its time, which the woman who set it down did not hear, the boy turning his head toward the note and getting no further, having no knuckle to set against it and no word to tell anybody, and filing it where a danger goes and not among the near-full answers because a note out of season is a fault in the handling and not a fourth likeness waiting to be promoted (T077, T061/T082); Jorra came through with dust in both forearms, kept dusty and clean ones on one tray, told the wheel it cared for steady hands and a pot it would do, knew its note three turns ahead and kept the knowing back from a kiln-hand on the wrong wind who said a pot singing early generally sings itself out early, and no third telling came of the counting room shut in the war though the man had a saying about a pot, a saying about a pot being not a saying about a room (T035/T031, T065/T095); a neighbour asking what the second day's firing wanted with the kiln newly open was sent to the pot and the weather with *Ask the weather. It's the weather's month. The house has said it*, the question arriving nowhere and leaving no entry, and the yard went around the two unswept lines called the thaw, the rack space with its broken birth-slates, the pan's gone ring and the field's empty place where a hand went in on Seed 15, with a look and a wide step and no number (T009/T013/T092, T098 carried); Tarn took the broom only after it had been instructed, six counted where the house could check and the seventh held where it could not, the ear-sentence brought to his teeth and no further, the breath drawn and not spent, no tenth stopping before the seventh year was earned and no collector coming up or down the clay road, his stroke over the flagstones going past the bright patch without the half-check so the Day 171 movement was not repeated (T036/T076); the loaf shelf kept its two unnamed gaps and its charcoal unread under a finger that did not go beneath it, the three near-full answers stood advanced and unpaid, six hearings lay beside and never among with the sixth from a person and no seventh invented, the widow's mark hung where it hung having been touched once before the kiln on the first morning and at no afternoon or yard since, and at the going of the light Sella carried him home up the clay road, woodsmoke before and river wind behind, set him on his bed and not in the Debt Loft, left the frozen thread unwiped, let her hand rest a half-beat longer than the setting needed, and went to her own bed while the two rooms did not touch; in the small hours the file was exact, complete and worth nothing at two paces, one hundred and eighty-two standing unspent in the dark of the wool, and nothing was joined, named, opened, called, spent or ended, the kiln month having opened without a boundary drawn and the danger keeping its order and no name and going unpaid.
Day 183 | Ember 4, Y.H. 813 (Fourthday) | the Vail kiln-house bed, hearth and loaf shelf's low angle, the Clay Yard and the kiln mouth, the kindling, the flagstones, the Debt Loft above the Vail wheel-shed, the clay road at the going of the light and the bed under the roof, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | The kiln month's second working week opened with the boy one hundred and eighty-three days old and six months and three days and nobody under any roof saying so, hot clay coming before the grey under tallow and wet wool with the sour mineral breath of the unswept corner, the kiln drawing breath in fours with a gap and the carried fire under the floor answering four low pulls with its fifth missing and its payer unnamed for a hundred and eighty-three days while the river's fifth low note and the boards' four nails, four and a thumb-width knot kept three counts in three places that did not become one (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); Sella Vail gave the instruction to the wool, the fire, the pot, the broom and the running water and never to the basket, said *the house* where another woman would say *I*, kept her half-pace, glanced once at the clay jar cold under its glaze without touching it, set the pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand with her hand resting a half-beat longer at the setting, and left the widow's mark untouched because a morning and a shelf are not openings (T058/T079); the yard went around the clean dark line, the finer grey called the thaw, the broken birth-slates under the lowest rack, the pan's gone round place and the empty place in the returning line where a hand went in on Seed 15, with a look and a wide step and no number (T009/T013/T092, T098 carried), while the danger kept an order and no name in hot clay, meal-fine dust and the sweetish lead edge off High Kiln and a pot put down wrong gave its early note which the boy filed as a danger and not a near-full answer (T077, T061/T082); then came the chapter's one meaningful change: in the Debt Loft Tarn took the warm stone Sella gave him in the winter Garl died out of its unmarked clay pot, held it, set it on the sill where the finger's-width opening let in light, looked at it a long while and put it back, no word, figure, payer or slate, neither reconciling the debt nor forgiving it (T006 advanced, not paid; T036/T076 advanced, not paid), and below the boy heard only a small dry scrape of a pot's foot set down on stone from the direction of the shed-loft and filed it on a place of its own, not joining it to the Day 161 damper fraction, the Day 166 held beat, the Day 171 flagstone half-check or any fourth likeness made a cause (T061/T082); Tarn told the kindling *Six counted. The next I don't count*, said *Yes, house* to an early thaw and a late while wanting the late because it puts off the paying, swept the collector's road without turning and brought the ear-sentence to the word that would call the loan with no figure and no name, drawing the breath he should have spent and not spending it while the nine stoppings stayed nine and no collector came (T036/T076); at the going of the light she carried him home up the clay road with woodsmoke before and river wind behind, set him on his bed and not in the Debt Loft and did not touch the mark again, and in the small hours he laid out the file as instruments on a towel, exact, complete and worth nothing at two paces, with one hundred and eighty-three standing exact and unspent in the dark of the wool.
Day 184 | Ember 5, Y.H. 813 (Fifthday) | the Vail wheel-shed and its finger's-width-open back room (not entered), the cold damper, the hearth, the loaf shelf and the kindling, the flagstones, the drying racks, the yard well and the clay road at the going of the light, the bed under the Vail roof, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | Three or four unnamed men went low up the Clay Yard before first grey and through the finger's-width door of the back room, and Sella Vail carried the boy past at the half-pace, turned no head, gave the house no name for them and did not say the room was cold, while the two tellings of a counting room shut in the war stood side by side and no third came (T035/T031, T065/T095); the boy was one hundred and eighty-four days old and six months and four days and nobody said so, the carried fire's payer was unnamed for a hundred and eighty-four days and the river's note and the boards' nails kept the other two counts in their places (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); Jorra Vail came through with fine grey slate dust ground small as meal into both forearms past the bent little finger and white in the creases where a fist had opened, not bone-coloured slip, kept dusty and clean forearms on one tray, asked neither which slate had been broken nor whether it was a birth-slate, spoke to the wheel before anyone (*It doesn't care whose hands. It cares if they're steady*), told a pot *You'll do*, knew its full note three turns before it left the wheel and withheld the knowing from a High Kiln man come up on the wrong wind with the sweetish edge of lead glaze at one mouth-corner who said a pot singing before its time generally sings itself out early, the knowing going to the near-full tray beside the hearings and never among them and no pot lost in the yard (T061/T082, T009/T013/T092); when Sella's voice went low and unfinished at the hearth his bent finger went to the cold iron of the damper and waited while her sentence reached the pot, then moved the tongue a hair's breadth and left the finger standing, the fraction remembered in the holding and not a change made again, not paired with the Day 166 beat beneath the shelf or the Day 171 flagstone half-check (T062), while Tarn's stroke went on over the bright patch without the half-check and over the road a house sends a man down when it wants a collector without once turning along it (T036/T076); the yard went around the clean dark line, the finer grey called the thaw, the birth-slates, the pan's gone place, the bare dark trough called weather and the empty place where a hand went in on Seed 15, and a neighbour with a wet pot was sent to the pot and the weather with *Ask the pot what a wet thing costs set down there. The house has said it*, carrying the question away level, while a rack-worker set a pot down wrong and did not hear its high thin note, which the boy filed where a danger goes and not among the near-full answers (T077, T058/T079, T098 carried); Sella kept her finger from beneath the loaf shelf so the charcoal waited unread (T100), Tarn brought the ear-sentence to the threshold unsaid and did not spend the Seed 16 breath, and the stone lay back in its pot in the loft, cold and unreconciled, the boy filing the remembering of the holding as a remembering and not a holding made again (T006 held, T036/T076); at the going of the light she carried him home up the clay road with woodsmoke before and river wind behind and set him on his bed and not in the Debt Loft, and in the small hours the file came back exact, complete and worthless, with one hundred and eighty-four standing exact and unspent in the dark of the wool.
Day 185 | Ember 6, Y.H. 813 (Sixthday) | the kiln mouth, the drying racks, the yard well, the flagstones, the wheel-shed edge and the cold damper, the loaf shelf, the kindling and the hearth under the Vail kiln-house roof, the clay road at the going of the light, the bed under the roof, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | The third working morning of the kiln month's second week came in without an edge to it and announced nothing, the boy one hundred and eighty-five days old and six months and five days and nobody under any roof saying so, the kiln drawing breath in fours with a gap and the carried fire under the floor answering four low pulls with the silence where a fifth should have been, its payer unnamed for a hundred and eighty-five days, while the river's fifth low note and the boards' four nails, four and a thumb-width knot kept three counts in three places that did not become one (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); the danger kept an order and no name that could not be carried into a chart --- hot clay under tallow, meal-fine slate dust white in a knuckle when a fist opened, the sweetish edge of lead glaze riding up from High Kiln on a wrong wind and a pot set down wrong giving its high thin note before its time, which the woman who set it down did not hear --- and the boy turned toward the note and filed it as a danger of the month, not a fourth near-full answer, with no pot lost, no lesson made and no edge paid (T077, T061/T082); Sella went around the clean dark line, the finer grey called the thaw, the birth-slates, the pan's gone ring, the bare dark trough and the empty place in the returning line with a look and a wide step and no number, gave the broom its instruction before she gave it to anybody, glanced once at the clay jar cold under its glaze without touching it, moved her lips along a loaf row with two unnamed gaps without giving a figure and kept her finger from beneath the front edge so the charcoal waited unread (T009/T013/T092, T098 carried, T058/T079, T100); Tarn took the broom only then, told the wood *Six counted. The next I don't count*, said *Yes, house* to an early thaw and a late while keeping the late because it puts off the paying, swept the collector's road without turning, brought the ear-sentence to the word that would call the loan with no figure and no name and did not spend the Seed 16 breath, the nine stoppings staying nine with no tenth before the seventh year was earned and no collector coming (T036/T076); Jorra worked at the wheel with slip dried to the colour of old bone past the bent finger, kept dusty and clean forearms on one tray, told a pot *You'll do*, knew its note three turns ahead and kept the knowing from the man on the wrong wind, and when Sella's sentence went low his bent finger stood in the cold damper until the words reached the pot, the fraction held as a remembered holding and not a new change (T035/T031, T062); the widow's mark hung where it hung, for a morning and a shelf are not openings and the one opening was before the kiln on Ember 1, and the two silences under one roof stayed unjoined (T058/T079); at the going of the light she carried him home up the clay road with woodsmoke before her in long low bars and river wind behind with the taste of water over water, went wide around the absences, set him on his bed and not in the Debt Loft, put the fold right without looking at his face, left the thread frozen, told the fire it was the thaw, said *The house will keep it*, counted under her breath toward no opening and did not touch the mark, and in the small hours he turned out the file as a man lays instruments on a towel --- six hearings beside and never among with the sixth from a person and no seventh in a hundred and eighty-five days, three near-full answers advanced and unpaid, the older intervals at Stone 13, 17 and 25, nine stoppings as objects and not a rule, the damper's two bites, three held changes on three places with the loft-sill sound on a fourth, two shut-room tellings and no third, the told bell beside the heard, the crust's gap, the shelf's two gaps, the pan's gone ring and the field's empty place on trays that do not touch --- wanting the crust, the breast, the pot handle two inches beyond his hand and a number spoken by somebody who would have known what a number counted and getting none, with one hundred and eighty-five standing exact, complete and unspent in the dark of the wool, nothing joined, named, opened, called, spent or ended, and the count going forward into Day 186, Ember 7.
Day 186 | Ember 7, Y.H. 813 (Seventhday) | the Vail kiln-house bed, hearth and loaf shelf's low angle, the Clay Yard, the kiln mouth, the drying racks, the kindling, the flagstones, the Vail wheel-shed edge, the Debt Loft rendered only as a sound from above and behind, the clay road at the going of the light, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | The middle days of the kiln month opened without a boundary drawn while the district kept calling Tallow the firing month and Ember the kiln month, the boy one hundred and eighty-six days old and six months and six days and nobody under any roof saying so, hot clay arriving around the door before the grey under tallow and wet wool with the sour mineral breath of the unswept corner, the kiln drawing breath in fours with a gap and the carried fire under the floor answering four low pulls with the silence where the fifth should have been, its payer unnamed for a hundred and eighty-six days, while the river's note of water over water through the finger's-width door and the boards' four nails, four and a thumb-width knot kept three counts in three places that did not become one (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); Sella Vail gave the instruction to the wool, the fire, the pot, the broom and the running water and never to the basket, said *the house* where another woman would say *I*, kept her half-pace, glanced once at the clay jar cold under its glaze without touching it, pushed coal-black hair back with the burn-wrist so the grey streak showed pale off the coals, set the pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand with her hand resting a half-beat longer at the setting, and left the widow's mark untouched, for a morning and a shelf are not openings and the one opening lay behind the household on Ember 1 (T058/T079); the yard went around the clean dark line, the finer grey called the thaw, the pan's gone round place, the bare dark trough whose water was called weather, the rack space with its broken birth-slates and the empty place in the returning line where a hand went in on Seed 15 and did not come out, with a look that asked for nothing and found nothing, a wide step and no number (T009/T013/T092, T098 carried); the danger kept an order and no name in hot clay, meal-fine slate dust white in a knuckle when a fist opens and the sweetish lead edge riding a wrong wind off High Kiln, and a rack-worker set a pot down wrong so that it gave its high thin note before its time, which the woman went on with her hands without hearing or making a lesson of, the boy filing the note where a danger goes and not as a fourth near-full answer, no pot lost and no payer named (T077, T061/T082); Tarn took the broom only after it had been instructed, told the kindling *Six counted. The next I don't count* with six in the worn thumb-mark where the house could check and the seventh at the lip where it could not beside Garl's unforged collector sentence, said *Yes, house* to an early thaw and a late while going on wanting the late because it puts off the paying, swept the collector's road without once turning along it and brought the ear-sentence to the word that would call the loan with no figure and no name, no collector coming (T036/T076); Jorra worked at the wheel with slip dried to the colour of old bone past the bent finger, parted dust from slip as a surgeon parts a clot from its vessel, told a pot *You'll do*, knew its full note and withheld it from a High Kiln kiln-master on the wrong wind who said a pot singing before its time generally sings itself out early, and when Sella's voice went low his bent finger went to the cold iron of the damper and held until her sentence reached the pot, a remembered holding not a change made again (T035/T031, T062, T065/T095), while the boy read the iron as one piece with two bites and no Grathic word for both mercies; at the going of the light she carried him home up the clay road with woodsmoke before and river wind behind, set him on his bed and not in the Debt Loft, left the thread frozen, told the fire it was the thaw, said *The house will keep it*, counted under her breath toward no opening and did not touch the mark, and in the small hours he laid out the file as instruments on a towel --- six hearings beside and never among with no seventh, three near-full answers advanced and unpaid, nine stoppings as objects and not a rule, three held changes on three places with the loft-sill scrape on a fourth --- wanting the crust, the breast, the pot handle and a number nobody would speak and getting none, with one hundred and eighty-six standing exact, complete and unspent in the dark of the wool and nothing joined, named, opened, called, spent or ended.
Day 187 | Ember 8, Y.H. 813 (Eighthday) | the yard well with its bare dark trough, the drying racks and the broken birth-slates beneath the lowest rack, the Vail wheel-shed and its finger's-width-open back room (entered by unnamed men, never by the narration), the cold damper, the loaf shelf, the kindling and the flagstones under the Vail kiln-house roof, the clay road at the going of the light, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | Three or four men went low through the finger's-width door of the back room before first grey and got no name, Sella Vail carrying past at the half-pace, turning no head, giving the house no name for them and not saying the room was cold, while the two tellings of a counting room shut in the war stood side by side with no third (T035/T031, T065/T095); the boy was one hundred and eighty-seven days old and six months and seven days and nobody said so, the carried fire's payer unnamed for a hundred and eighty-seven days and the river's note and the boards' nails keeping the other two counts in their places (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); Jorra came through with fine grey slate dust ground small as meal into both forearms past the bent little finger and white in the creases where a fist had opened, not bone-coloured slip, kept dusty forearms and clean ones on one tray, asked neither which slate had been broken nor whether it was a birth-slate, spoke to the wheel before anyone (*It doesn't care whose hands. It cares if they're steady*), told a pot *You'll do*, knew its note three turns ahead and withheld the knowing from a High Kiln kiln-master on the wrong wind who said a pot singing early generally sings itself out early, the knowing going to the near-full tray beside the hearings and never among them, and when Sella's voice went low his bent finger stood in the cold iron until her sentence reached the pot, a remembered holding not a change made again, not paired with the Day 166 beat or the Day 171 half-check (T061/T082, T062); at the well a glaze-room hand come up from High Kiln waited with the sweetish edge of lead glaze on his breath, and when he drank the pale line showed wet white at his gum and he kept one hand curled at his side rather than using it, setting the dipper down with care because his fingers had stopped telling him where it was, while the boy read the line, the hand, the pewter-pale nails, the short breath with its small pause and the tremor in the wrist from two paces in the surgeon's register, exact, complete and worth nothing at two paces, and filed the whole reading where a danger of the month goes --- not among the six hearings, not a seventh, not a fourth near-full answer, no name, figure, cause or payer (T077, T061/T082); the yard went around its two unswept lines, the birth-slates, the pan's gone place and the empty place in the returning line with a look and a wide step and no number (T009/T013/T092, T098 carried), and a neighbour come up from the tubs with a wet pot asking what the firing cost now the kiln month's middle days were on was answered after one glance at the cold glazed jar and a hand shut half over nothing with *Ask the pot what a wet thing costs set down there. The house has said it*, the question carried away level with nothing slopped, priced, refused or entered (T058/T079, T077); Tarn took the broom only after it was instructed, said *Six counted. The next I don't count*, went over the shallow bright patch on the flagstones without the half-check so the Day 171 change stayed where it was laid, swept the collector's road without turning and no collector came (T036/T076); at the going of the light she carried him home up the clay road with woodsmoke before and river wind behind, set him on his bed and not in the Debt Loft, put the fold right without looking at his face, left the thread frozen, told the fire it was the thaw, said *The house will keep it*, counted under her breath toward no opening and did not touch the mark, and in the small hours he added the reading of the body at the well to his file on a place of its own, exact and worth nothing, not a hearing, not a seventh, not a stopping, not one of the three held changes and not a fourth likeness stood up to call itself a cause, with one hundred and eighty-seven standing exact, complete and unspent in the dark of the wool and nothing joined, named, opened, called, spent or ended.
Day 188 | Ember 9, Y.H. 813 (Ninthday) | the Clay Yard, the kiln mouth, the drying racks, the yard well and the bare dark trough, the flagstones, the loaf shelf, the kindling, the hearth and the bed under the Vail kiln-house roof, the clay road up which the carried man was taken, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | The ninth morning came up out of the clay without a boundary drawn, the boy one hundred and eighty-eight days old and six months and eight days and nobody under any roof saying so, the carried fire's payer unnamed for a hundred and eighty-eight days and the river's fifth low note and the boards' four nails, four and a thumb-width knot keeping three counts in three places that did not become one (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); the sweetish lead edge rode a wrong wind off High Kiln and slate dust ground small as meal lay white in the creases of a knuckle when a fist opened, and a rack-worker set a pot down wrong so that it gave its high thin note before its time and then sang itself out early, the woman going on with her hands and making no lesson of it, the boy filing the sound where a danger goes and not as a fourth near-full answer, the High Kiln saying not made true as a lesson and no pot lost (T077, T061/T082); then two men came out of the kiln with the glaze-room hand between them, one with a thumb hooked under his arm to keep the head from lopping and the other at the feet, carrying him breathing across the yard toward the clay road in the long undignified interval of a body still alive, the sweet-sickness going before him, the pale line wet white at his gum, his own hand held limp with fingers the colour of old rope and nails pewter-pale, while the yard went around the place they crossed the way it goes around a barrow set down wrong, a woman with a wet pot watching and going on with her pot and a man with a barrow easing his load round without turning his head, nobody giving the carriers a road, a figure or a word; Sella set the basket down at the yard's edge, turned the boy's face as she would turn a pot so the light fell where she wanted it, went wide around the place the carriers crossed with a look that asked for nothing and found nothing, gave the basket no account of it and the yard no name for what ailed him, her hand shutting half over nothing at the clay jar and the widow's mark hanging where it hung, for a yard and a carrying are not openings (T058/T079); from two paces the boy read the carried body as a man with lead in him --- the line at the gum, the sweet breath, the held hand, the thumb under the arm, the colour of the nail beds, the short breath and its pause --- exact, complete and worth nothing at two paces, unable to reach the hand, speak the reading or lift his own chin, the only adult in the yard and the least use in it, the gift that could say *leave it* in another country buying nothing here (T077); no collector was called, no figure given, nobody said what ailed the man, no lesson was made of the glaze, no payer named to the keeping and no slate opened to receive the cost, the man going out breathing and on up the clay road, a breath kept not an ending (T077); at the going of the light she carried him home up the clay road with woodsmoke before her in long low bars and river wind behind with the taste of water over water, went wide around the pan's gone place, the bare dark trough, the rack space and the empty place in the returning line, set him on his bed and not in the Debt Loft, put the fold right without looking at his face, left the thread frozen, told the fire it was the thaw, said *The house will keep it*, counted under her breath toward no opening and did not touch the mark, while the pot that had sung itself out early was swept around and not made an omen; in the small hours he laid out the file as instruments on a towel --- six hearings beside and never among with the sixth from a person and no seventh, three near-full answers advanced and unpaid, the older intervals and nine stoppings as objects and not a rule, the damper's double bite, three held changes on three places with the loft-sill scrape on a fourth --- and laid the carried body's pale line on a place of its own, exact and worth nothing, not joined to the sweet wind, the dust, the early note or any absence, wanting the crust, the breast, the pot handle two inches beyond his hand and a number nobody would speak and getting none, with one hundred and eighty-eight standing exact, complete and unspent in the dark of the wool, the cost of the man carried out entered nowhere and the count going forward into Day 189, Ember 10.
Day 189 | Ember 10, Y.H. 813 (Tenthday, the first Account-day and market-day of Ember) | under the Vail kiln-house roof at the hearth, loaf shelf and bed, the Clay Yard and the clay road, the Cross, the moot-post's finger's width of shade, the reading block, the pot board and stalls, the north bridge and the out-of-line landing, the market's edge, the clay road home, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | The kiln month's first Account-day and market-day came up over the Cross with the boy one hundred and eighty-nine days old and six months and nine days and nobody under any roof saying so, hot clay under tallow and wet wool with the sour breath of the unswept corner, the kiln drawing breath in fours with a gap and not a bell until the square's bell went four, four and a gap with nobody crossing himself while the heard bell was kept beside the told (T071/T072, T061/T082, T088), and Sella Vail carried him down at the half-pace from every open slate, instruction to the wool and the river wind and never the basket, and set him in the moot-post's thin shade where no slate lay, standing half a pace off through the reading (T058/T079); Berr Clay came up short off the clay road with his office cord folded square under his coat and green of tasted lead glaze at one mouth-corner, wiped his thumb twice before touching nothing, passed a finger over a figure and read one in twelve, as the notice says, low into the crowd, called the levy and denied it with The levy is called. It is not called by me and your house, never I, and went off toward High Kiln with the box locked and the true subtraction inside, believing he had done a kindness, so that the month's arithmetic changed where two hundred people stood and could not be changed back, the printed figure stood unchallenged and a figure read aloud was still not the figure paid (T015/T041/T047/T077); before noon Hessa Rale carried his narrow slate up the road to Greybank edge-on to her forearm with the writing to her sleeve, hand to slate before eyes to crowd, answering the question put and not the one behind it with I don't read it. I carry it. There's a difference, and the road doesn't care which and Cross to Greybank. Reading goes up before noon. I've got a road to walk, and no figure came off it (T080 advanced, not paid), while the soft-cuffed clerk stayed set at the out-of-line landing for a measure nobody called with the market selling over him as water over a stone (T090), a woman at the pot board turned a cup a quarter and set it back with the sixth green still a colour joined to no ear, and the foundling stood at the market's edge with a green sliver and no loaf, slate, sale or dialogue, the sixth sighting still sixth and not spent (T050/T051, T033/T034 held); the reading went twice over the basket and did not answer the man carried out on Day 188, no figure, collector or slate knowing what his carrying cost and the pale line at his gum lying on a place of its own (T064), and at the going of the light she carried him home going wide around the pan's gone ring, the bare dark trough, the broken birth-slates, the field's empty place and the place the carriers crossed, with a look and a wide step and no number (T009/T013/T092/T098), set him on the bed and not the Debt Loft with the fold right and the thread unwiped, did not touch the mark because a reading and a dusk are not openings, and in the small hours turned out the file --- six hearings with no seventh, three near-full answers unpaid, nine stoppings with no tenth, three held changes on three places, the loft scrape on a fourth, the pale line on a fifth, told bell beside heard and told figure beside paid --- so that one hundred and eighty-nine stood exact, complete and unspent and the count went forward into Day 190.
Day 190 | Ember 11, Y.H. 813 (Firstday) | under the Vail kiln-house roof at the bed, hearth, loaf shelf and kindling, the Clay Yard, the yard well and the bare trough, the drying racks, the wheel-shed edge, the finger's-width-open back room (not entered), the cold damper, the flagstones, the Debt Loft (heard only), Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | The account had gone up the clay road in Berr's locked box in the dark and no hand under the Vail roof could reach the true subtraction back out, the printed one in twelve standing unchallenged and a figure said over a basket still not a figure paid, while the boy came into his hundred and ninetieth morning one hundred and ninety days old and six months and ten days and told by nobody, the carried fire missing its fifth with its payer unnamed for a hundred and ninety days and the river, the boards and the fire keeping three counts in three places that did not become one (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); hot clay came ahead of the grey and Sella Vail gave the instruction to wool, fire, pot, broom and running water and never the basket, said the house, kept her half-pace, glanced once at the clay jar cold under its glaze without touching it, fed him barley gruel from the house's own dish with two fingers behind his ear, wiped the spoon on her sleeve and not his mouth, set the pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand with her hand resting a half-beat longer at the setting, and left the widow's mark where it hung because a morning and a shelf are not openings (T058/T079), and a woman who asked what the month would cost now the reading had gone up was sent to the pot and the weather with Ask the pot what a wet thing costs set down there. The house has said it, and carried the question away level with nothing priced, refused or entered (T077); three or four unnamed men went low into the finger's-width-open back room before first grey and got no name, the two shut-room tellings staying two with no third (T035/T031, T065/T095); Jorra Vail came through with fine grey slate dust ground small as meal into both forearms past the bent little finger and under the nails, white where a fist had opened and not bone-coloured slip, kept dusty and clean forearms on one tray, spoke to the wheel before anyone with It doesn't care whose hands. It cares if they're steady, told a pot You'll do, knew its full note three turns ahead and withheld it from a man come up on the wrong wind, no pot lost and no lesson made, and held his bent finger in the cold damper until Sella's unfinished sentence reached the pot as a remembered holding and not a change made again (T061/T082, T062); Tarn took the broom only after it was instructed, told the kindling Six counted. The next I don't count with six in the worn thumb-mark and the seventh at the lip beside Garl's unforged collector sentence, Ash worked into his knuckles and the pale clip-ring at his wrist, said Yes, house to an early thaw and a late while wanting the late because it puts off the paying, swept the collector's road without turning with no collector coming, and brought the ear-sentence to the word that would call the loan and stopped there with no figure and no name, nine stoppings still nine, no tenth before the seventh year was earned, the Seed 16 breath unspent and the loan uncalled (T036/T076); the stone lay back in its unmarked pot in the loft cold and unreconciled by not trying, which is not forgiving, and the charcoal waited unread beneath the shelf with Sella's finger kept from beneath the front edge (T006, T100), and in the small hours the carried man's pale line lay on a place of its own while one hundred and ninety stood exact, complete and unspent and the count went forward.
Day 191 | Ember 12, Y.H. 813 (Secondday) | under the Vail kiln-house roof at the bed, hearth, loaf shelf and kindling, the Clay Yard, the yard well and the bare trough, the drying racks, the wheel-shed edge and its finger's-width-open back room (not entered), the cold damper, the flagstones, the Debt Loft (heard only), the clay road at the going of the light, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | The kiln month's second working week opened without anybody drawing a boundary under the first while the district kept calling Tallow the firing month and Ember the kiln month and neither was corrected, the boy one hundred and ninety-one days old and six months and eleven days and nobody saying so, the carried fire answering four low pulls and missing its fifth with its payer unnamed for a hundred and ninety-one days and the river's fifth and the boards' knot keeping the other two counts in three places that did not become one (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); the crust lay leathery two inches from his ear with its empty place unnamed and no third, the drool thread frozen and unwiped, cold stopped at cheek, temple and the small bare joint at the wrist where the wool rode back, and the afterdrop waited on a warming nobody in this house would perform, while Sella gave the instruction to wool, fire, pot, broom, running water and river wind and never the basket, said the house, kept her half-pace, glanced once at the clay jar and shut her hand half over nothing, pushed coal-black hair back with the burn-wrist so the grey streak showed pale off the coals, set the pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand, and left the mark hanging because there had been no opening since before the kiln on Ember 1 (T058/T079); a rack woman with slip dried pale in her wrists asked what the pots would want before the tend was out and was told to ask the pot what a wet thing costs set down there, and the sweetish edge of lead glaze came up from High Kiln on a wrong wind and went out on the same wind as a direction answered with pot and weather, the pot that had sung itself out early standing swept around and not made an omen and the kiln-master's saying not made true as a lesson so soon after the carrying-out (T077, T064/T061/T082); the yard went around the clean dark line and the finer grey called the thaw, the broken birth-slates, the pan's gone round place, the bare dark trough, the field's empty place and the place the carriers crossed, each with a look and a wide step and no number, and the back room kept its finger's width of open with the two shut-room tellings still two (T009/T013/T092/T098, T035/T031); Jorra kept dusty and clean forearms on one tray, told a pot You'll do and withheld its note from the wrong wind, and held his bent finger in the cold damper until Sella's unfinished sentence reached the pot as a remembered holding, while Tarn took the broom after instruction, counted six where the house could check and held the seventh at the lip beside Garl's sentence, brought the ear-sentence to the word that would call the loan and did not spend it, nine stoppings still nine with no tenth, the Seed 16 breath unspent and the loan uncalled, his stroke going over the flagstones and the shallow bright patch without the half-check (T036/T076, T062); the stone lay cold and unreconciled in its loft pot, the boy filing only the remembering of the small dry scrape on a place of its own (T006), and at the going of the light she carried him home going wide around the same turns and set him on the bed with the fold right, the thread frozen and the two rooms not touching; in the small hours the file was laid out with six hearings beside and never among, five from four sources and the sixth out of Dessa Silt from a person, no seventh in a hundred and ninety-one days, three near-full answers advanced and unpaid, the older intervals and nine stoppings as objects with no tenth, the damper's double bite, three held changes on three places, the loft scrape on a fourth, the two shut-room tellings side by side with no third, told bell beside heard and told figure beside paid, the pale line at the gum on a fifth place of its own exact, complete and worth nothing at two paces and not joined to the sweet wind, the dust, the early note, the sixth green, the foundling's sliver, the pan's ring or any absence, the sixth green still a colour joined to no ear and the foundling a shape with no sale whose head went half a beat toward a set-down pot and then stopped (T064, T050/T051, T033/T034), so that one hundred and ninety-one stood exact, complete and unspent in the dark of the wool, nothing joined, named, opened, called, spent or ended, and the count went forward into Day 192, Ember 13.
Day 192 | Ember 13, Y.H. 813 (Thirdday) | under the Vail kiln-house roof at the bed, hearth, loaf shelf's low angle and kiln mouth, the Clay Yard with the trough at the yard well, the drying racks and the lowest rack, the wall where the shallow pan stood, the field's empty place and the place the carriers crossed, the wheel-shed edge, the kindling, the flagstones and the clay road, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | The hand outside the wool went up an inch toward the river's note of water over water through the finger's-width door and came down against the fold as it had every morning of his life while the boy came into his hundred and ninety-second day, six months and twelve days, told by nobody, the carried fire answering four low pulls with its fifth missing and its payer unnamed for a hundred and ninety-two days and the river's fifth note, the boards' four nails, four and a thumb-width knot keeping three counts in three places that did not become one (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); hot clay came under tallow and wet wool under that with the sour breath of the unswept corner, the crust gone leathery two inches from his ear with its empty place unnamed and no third, the drool thread frozen and unwiped, cold stopped at cheek, temple and the bare wrist joint, and the afterdrop waiting on a warming nobody would perform, while Sella Vail gave the instruction to the wool, the fire, the pot, the broom and the running water and never the basket, said the house, kept her half-pace, glanced once at the clay jar cold under its glaze without touching it, pushed coal-black hair back with the burn-wrist so the grey streak showed pale, set the pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand with her hand resting a half-beat longer at the setting, told the fire it was the thaw, counted under her breath toward no opening, and left the widow's mark untouched because a quiet morning after a reading was not an opening (T058/T079); a neighbour asking what the firing would want now the account had gone up the road was sent to the pot and the weather with Ask the pot what a wet thing costs set down there. The house has said it, going away level with nothing priced, refused or entered (T077), while the printed figure stood at one in twelve unchallenged, the true subtraction sat locked in Berr's box up the road, Hessa's slate had gone to Greybank unread and the soft-cuffed clerk remained set at the out-of-line landing for an uncalled measure (T015/T041/T047, T080, T090 held); the yard went around the clean dark line and the finer grey called the thaw, the broken birth-slates beneath the lowest rack, the pan's gone round ring, the bare dark trough whose water was called weather, the field's empty place where a hand went in on Seed 15 and the place the carriers crossed, each with a look and a wide step and no number, while Tarn took the broom only after it was instructed and went over the flagstones without the half-check, Jorra's wheel turned beyond the shed and the back room kept its finger's width of open with no name given to the men who had gone in before first grey (T009/T013/T092/T098, T036/T076, T035/T031/T065/T095); at the shelf Sella moved her lips along the loaf row with two unnamed gaps and gave nobody a figure, kept her finger from beneath the front edge so the charcoal stayed unread, and at the going of the light carried him home up the clay road with woodsmoke before and river wind behind, set him on his bed and not in the Debt Loft, left the thread frozen and did not touch the mark, and in the small hours he turned out the file as instruments on a towel --- six hearings beside and never among with the sixth from a person and no seventh in a hundred and ninety-two days, three near-full answers advanced and unpaid, nine stoppings as objects with no tenth, three held changes on three places with the loft scrape on a fourth and the carried man's pale line on a fifth, the told bell beside the heard and the told figure beside the paid --- exact, complete and worth nothing, with one hundred and ninety-two standing unspent in the dark of the wool.
Day 193 | Ember 14, Y.H. 813 (Fourthday) | under the Vail kiln-house roof at the bed, hearth, loaf shelf's low angle and kiln mouth, the drying racks, the yard well and the bare trough, the wheel-shed edge, the back room (not entered), the kindling, the flagstones, the Debt Loft (heard only), the clay road at the going of the light, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | The kiln month's second working week ran on without a boundary drawn and neither the district's name of firing month for Tallow nor its name of kiln month for Ember was corrected, while the boy came into his hundred and ninety-third day, six months and thirteen days, told by nobody, the carried fire's payer without a name for a hundred and ninety-three days and the river, fire and boards keeping three counts in three places that did not become one (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); hot clay came under tallow and wet wool under that with the sour breath of the unswept corner, the crust leathery two inches from his ear, the thread frozen and unwiped, the afterdrop waiting past a warming nobody would perform, while Sella Vail fed him barley gruel from the house's own dish with two fingers behind his ear, wiped the spoon on her sleeve and not his mouth, gave the instruction to the wool, fire, pot, broom and running water and never the basket, said the house, kept her half-pace, glanced once at the clay jar without touching it, set the pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand and left the widow's mark where it hung because a morning and a shelf are not openings (T058/T079); a rack woman with slip dried pale in her wrists asked what the pots would want before the kiln month was out and was sent to the pot and the weather with nothing priced or entered, and the sweet edge of lead glaze came up off High Kiln on a wrong wind again, was taken as a direction and not a name or a cause and went out on the same wind, nobody naming what ailed the man carried out on Day 188, calling a collector, giving a figure, opening a slate or making a lesson of the glaze, so that his pale line lay on a place of its own and his cost stood entered nowhere (T077, T064); three or four men unnamed went low into the finger's-width-open back room before first grey and got no name, the two shut-room tellings staying two, and the yard went around the broken birth-slates, the dark line, the thaw, the pan's gone ring, the bare trough and the field's empty place with a look and a wide step (T035/T031/T065/T095, T009/T013/T092/T098); Jorra came through with grey slate dust ground small as meal past the bent finger and white in the creases where a fist had opened, not bone-coloured slip, kept dusty and clean forearms on one tray, told the wheel it did not care whose hands and a pot it would do, withheld the full note he knew three turns ahead from a man on a wrong wind who said a pot singing early generally sings itself out early, and held his bent finger in the cold damper until Sella's unfinished sentence reached the pot --- a remembered holding and not a change made again (T035/T031, T061/T082, T062); Tarn took the broom only after it was instructed, told the wood Six counted. The next I don't count with six in the worn thumb-mark and the seventh at the lip beside Garl's unforged collector sentence, Ash worked into his knuckles and the pale clip-ring at his wrist, said Yes, house to an early thaw and a late while wanting the late, brought the ear-sentence all the way to the word that would call the loan and stopped there, drew the Seed 16 breath and did not spend it, kept the nine stoppings with no tenth and went over the bright patch without the half-check, and no collector came up or down the clay road (T036/T076), while the stone lay cold and unreconciled in its loft pot and the charcoal waited unread (T006, T100); then came the chapter's first small movement, for at the low angle under the shelf where the underside made a roof that stopped at his reach his hand outside the wool went up an inch toward the note of water over water and did not come down at once but held for the length of the small pause in the kiln's breath before the next pull, a fraction filed on a place of its own --- not a hearing, not a seventh, not a stopping, not one of the three held changes, not a payment, with no payer named and no cause allowed to stand up out of it (T071/T072, T061/T082); the told bell stood beside the heard and the told figure beside the paid, no third gap came and no finger of his drew the line, and one hundred and ninety-three stood exact, complete and unspent in the dark of the wool, the hand lying outside the wool with the inch still in it.
Day 194 | Ember 15, Y.H. 813 (Fifthday) | under the Vail kiln-house roof at the bed, hearth, loaf shelf's low angle and kiln mouth, the yard well and the bare trough, the drying racks and the space beneath the lowest rack, the wall where the shallow pan stood, the field's empty place, the place the carriers crossed, the wheel-shed and its finger's-width-open back room (not entered), the clay road at the going of the light, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | The third quiet morning was the fullest yet and the kiln month's second working week drew toward its end with no boundary drawn, the boy one hundred and ninety-four days old and six months and fourteen days and nobody saying so, the carried fire's payer unnamed for a hundred and ninety-four days and the river's fifth note, the boards' four nails, four and a thumb-width knot and the fire's four pulls keeping three counts in three places that did not become one (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); hot clay came under tallow and wet wool with the sour corner breath, the crust leathery two inches from his ear, the thread frozen and unwiped, cold stopped at cheek, temple and the bare wrist joint, and in the morning his hand went up an inch toward the note of water over water and came down at once with no holding in it yet, while Sella Vail gave the instruction to the wool, the fire, the pot, the broom, the running water, the river wind and never the basket, said the house, kept her half-pace, glanced once at the clay jar without touching it, fed him barley gruel from the house's own dish, wiped the spoon on her sleeve and not his mouth, set the pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand with her hand resting a half-beat longer, counted under her breath toward no opening and left the widow's mark untouched because there had been no opening since before the kiln on Ember 1 (T058/T079); a woman with a wet pot asked what a wet thing cost set down in the dry heat and a man come off the clay road asked what the kiln would want now the figure had gone up, and both were sent to the pot and the weather, going away level with nothing priced, refused or entered while the printed one in twelve stood unchallenged and the true subtraction sat in a locked box up the road (T077, T015/T041/T047); the yard went around the clean dark line and the thaw, the broken birth-slates, the pan's gone round ring, the bare trough called weather, the field's empty place and the place the carriers crossed, on trays that did not touch, the sweet edge riding up off High Kiln on a wrong wind as a direction and going out on it with no man revived as a cause, no collector called and no Grathic sickness named (T009/T013/T092/T098, T064); three or four men unnamed went low into the back room before first grey and got no name, Jorra kept dusty and clean forearms on one tray, withheld his note from the wrong wind and held his bent finger in the cold damper as a remembered repetition, and Tarn kept six where the house could check and the seventh at the lip, brought the ear-sentence to the word that would call the loan, held the Seed 16 breath, kept nine stoppings with no tenth and swept past the bright patch without the half-check so that no collector came (T035/T031/T062/T065/T095, T036/T076), while the charcoal waited unread, the stone lay cold in its loft pot and the file went with him and came back exact and worth nothing (T100, T006, T060/T086/T097); at the going of the light she carried him home up the clay road with woodsmoke before and river wind behind, went wide around the absences, set him on his bed and not in the Debt Loft, put the fold right without looking at his face, left the thread frozen, told the fire it was the thaw, banked the coals and did not touch the mark; then in the small hours, after the file came back exact, complete and worthless with six hearings beside and never among, no seventh, three near-full answers unpaid, nine stoppings with no tenth, three held changes on three places with the loft scrape on a fourth and the carried man's pale line on a fifth, his hand outside the wool went up an inch toward the note of water over water through the finger's-width door and **stayed up for the length of one of the breaths Sella counted at her fire in the room that did not touch this one** --- the small intake at the top, the hold, the letting go --- before coming down against the fold, an inch held and let go, filed on a place of its own beside the others, not a hearing, not a seventh, not a stopping, not a payment, not one of the three held changes, with no payer named and no doctrine allowed to stand up out of it, while he still could not close two inches on the pot handle, lift his chin to the crust or speak a number, the body humiliating him and moving by an inch at the same time, and one hundred and ninety-four standing exact, complete and unspent in the dark of the wool with nothing joined, named, opened, called, spent or ended, the count going forward into Day 195, Ember 16 (T071/T072, T061/T082, T088).
Day 195 | Ember 16, Y.H. 813 (Sixthday) | under the Vail kiln-house roof at the bed, hearth and loaf shelf's low angle, the Clay Yard, the kiln mouth, the drying racks, the yard well and the trough, the wall where the shallow pan stood, the field's empty place, the place the carriers crossed, the wheel-shed edge, the kindling, the flagstones and the clay road at the going of the light, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | The hand outside the wool went up an inch toward the river's note of water over water and came down as it had every morning of his life while the boy came into his hundred and ninety-fifth day, six months and fifteen days, told by nobody, the carried fire answering four low pulls with its fifth missing and its payer unnamed for a hundred and ninety-five days and the river's fifth note, the kiln's four-and-a-gap and the boards' four nails, four and a thumb-width knot keeping three counts in three places that did not become one (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); hot clay came under tallow and wet wool under that with the sour corner breath, the crust leathery two inches from his ear, the thread frozen and unwiped, cold stopped at cheek, temple and the bare wrist joint, the afterdrop waiting on a warming nobody would perform, while Sella Vail gave the instruction to the wool, the fire, the pot, the broom and the running water and never the basket, said the house, kept her half-pace, glanced once at the clay jar cold under its glaze without touching it, pushed coal-black hair back with the burn-wrist so the grey streak showed pale, fed him barley gruel from the house's own dish with two fingers behind his ear and the spoon wiped on her sleeve and not his mouth, set the pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand with her hand resting a half-beat longer at the setting, told the fire it was the thaw, counted under her breath toward no opening and left the widow's mark untouched because a morning and a shelf are not openings and there had been none since before the kiln on Ember 1 (T058/T079); a neighbour with dry mud-scurf on her hem asked what the firing would want now the account had gone up the road and was sent to the pot and the weather with Ask the pot what a firing costs set down there. The next I don't count, brought the ear-sentence to the word that would call the loan and stopped, drew the Seed 16 breath and did not spend it, kept the nine stoppings with no tenth before the seventh year was earned and went over the bright patch without the half-check so that no collector came (T036/T076), and Jorra kept dusty and clean forearms on one tray, told a pot You'll do, withheld his note from a man on the wrong wind and held his bent finger in the cold damper until Sella's unfinished sentence reached the pot as a remembered repetition (T035/T031/T062); at the going of the light she carried him home up the clay road with woodsmoke before and river wind behind, set him on his bed and not in the Debt Loft, put the fold right without looking at his face, left the thread frozen and did not touch the mark, and in the small hours the file came back exact, complete and worth nothing with six hearings beside and never among and no seventh, three near-full answers unpaid, the older intervals and nine stoppings as objects with no tenth, three held changes on three places with the loft scrape on a fourth and the carried man's pale line on a fifth, then his hand outside the wool went up an inch toward the note of water over water and, when he asked it to stay, **held for the length of one of the breaths Sella counted at her fire in the room that does not touch this one** --- the small intake, the hold, the letting go --- before coming down against the fold, an inch held and let go, filed on a place of its own, not a hearing, not a seventh, not a stopping, not a payment, not one of the three held changes, with no payer named and no doctrine stood up out of it, while he still could not close two inches on the pot handle, lift his chin to the crust or speak a number, the body humiliating him and moving by an inch at the same time, and one hundred and ninety-five standing exact, complete and unspent in the dark of the wool (T071/T072, T061/T082).

three or four unnamed men went low into the finger's-width-open back room before first grey and got no name, the two shut-room tellings staying two (T035/T031/T065/T095), and Jorra came through with fine grey slate dust ground small as meal into both forearms past the bent little finger and white in the creases where a fist had opened, not bone-coloured slip, parted dust from slip as a surgeon parts a clot from its vessel, kept dusty and clean forearms on one tray, told the wheel it did not care whose hands and a pot it would do, withheld the full note he knew three turns ahead from a High Kiln kiln-master on a wrong wind who said a pot singing early generally sings itself out early, and held his bent finger in the cold damper until Sella's unfinished sentence reached the pot as a remembered repetition (T035/T031/T062, T065/T095); at the racks a woman set a pot down wrong so that it gave its high thin note before its time and sang itself out early, and she went on with her hands, set it square, swept around the fault and made no lesson of it, the boy filing the note where a danger goes and not among the hearings, not as a seventh and not as a cause, with no collector called, no figure given and no second edge paid (T077, T061/T082, T064 held); at the kindling Tarn took the broom only after instruction, told the wood Six counted. the stone lay back in its unmarked pot in the loft cold and unreconciled by not trying, which is not forgiving, and the small dry scrape of a pot's foot on loft stone lay on its own fourth place (T006), while the charcoal waited unread beneath the shelf (T100); at the going of the light she carried him home up the clay road with woodsmoke before and river wind behind, went wide around the absences, set him on his bed and not in the Debt Loft, put the fold right without looking at his face, left the thread frozen and did not touch the mark, and in the small hours the file came back exact, complete and worth nothing with six hearings beside and never among and no seventh in a hundred and ninety-six days, three near-full answers unpaid, nine stoppings with no tenth, three held changes on three places, the loft scrape on a fourth and the pale line on a fifth, then the hand outside the wool went up an inch toward the note of water over water and came down **before the counted breath had finished** --- the body keeping its own time, an inch held briefly no more a reaching than an inch held long and an inch let go early no more a failure than an inch that stays --- the shorter holding filed on its own place and not joined to the breath that outlasted it, to the fire's gap or to any other place, not a seventh, hearing, stopping, payment or cause, with no payer named, while he still could not close two inches, lift the crust or speak a number, and one hundred and ninety-six stood exact, complete and unspent in the dark of the wool with nothing joined, named, opened, called, spent or ended (T071/T072, T061/T082).
Day 197 | Ember 18, Y.H. 813 (Eighthday) | under the Vail kiln-house roof at the bed, hearth, loaf shelf and kiln mouth, the drying racks, the yard well and the bare trough, the wall where the shallow pan stood, the field's empty place, the place the carriers crossed, the wheel-shed and its finger's-width-open back room (not entered), the cold damper, the kindling, the flagstones, the Debt Loft (heard only), the clay road at the going of the light, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | The kiln month's second working week drew toward its end with no boundary drawn, the boy one hundred and ninety-seven days old and six months and seventeen days and nobody under any roof saying so, the carried fire's payer unnamed for a hundred and ninety-seven days and the river's fifth note, the kiln's four-and-a-gap and the boards' four nails, four and a thumb-width knot keeping three counts in three places that did not become one (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); Sella Vail gave the instruction to the wool, the fire, the pot, the broom, the running water and the river wind and never the basket, said the house, kept her half-pace, glanced once at the clay jar cold under its glaze without touching it, kept her finger from beneath the shelf so the charcoal stayed unread, fed him barley gruel from the house's own dish with the spoon wiped on her sleeve and not his mouth, pushed coal-black hair back with the burn-wrist so the grey streak showed pale, set the pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand with the half-beat at the setting, counted under her breath toward no opening and left the widow's mark untouched, for a morning was not an opening and there had been none since before the kiln on Ember 1 (T058/T079, T100); three or four unnamed men with collars high went low-voiced into the back room before first grey and got no name, the two shut-room tellings staying two (T035/T031/T065/T095), and Jorra came through with grey slate dust ground small as meal into both forearms past the bent finger and white in the creases where a fist had opened, kept dusty and clean forearms on one tray, told the wheel it did not care whose hands and a pot it would do, withheld the knowing from a man come up on a wrong wind who said a pot singing before its time generally sings itself out early --- the saying staying a saying about a pot and not made true as a lesson, no pot lost --- and held his bent finger in the cold damper until Sella's unfinished sentence reached the pot as a remembered repetition (T035/T031/T062, T065/T095); at the kiln mouth the sweet edge of lead glaze rode up off High Kiln on a wrong wind and went out on the same wind as a direction and not a second edge paid, no collector called for the glaze-room hand carried out while the month was young and his pale line at his gum lying on a place of its own, exact, complete and worth nothing at two paces, with no offstage fate invented for him (T064 held); neighbours asking what the firing would want now the account had gone up the road were sent to the pot and the weather and went away level, the printed one in twelve standing unchallenged, Berr's box gone up the road locked with the true subtraction inside, Hessa's narrow slate unread at Greybank and the soft-cuffed clerk still set at the out-of-line landing for a measure nobody called (T077, T015/T041/T047/T080/T090 held); the yard went around the clean dark line and the thaw, the rack space with its broken birth-slates, the pan's gone round ring, the bare dark trough, the field's empty place and the place the carriers crossed with a look and a wide step and no number, no third gap coming and no fourth likeness made a cause (T009/T013/T092/T098); at the going of the light she carried him home up the clay road with woodsmoke before and river wind behind and went wide around the absences, set him on his bed and not in the Debt Loft, put the fold right without a look at his face, left the thread frozen, banked the coals with the back of a broken pot and did not touch the mark because a dusk is not an opening, and in the small hours he turned out the file as a man lays instruments on a towel --- six hearings beside and never among with the sixth from a person and no seventh in a hundred and ninety-seven days, three near-full answers advanced and unpaid, nine stoppings with no tenth, the told bell beside the heard and the told figure beside the paid, three held changes on three places with the loft scrape on a fourth and the pale line on a fifth, the crust's gap, the shelf's two gaps, the pan's gone ring and the field's empty place on trays that do not touch, the warm stone cold in its loft pot, the Seed 16 breath unspent and the loan uncalled --- and then the hand outside the wool went up an inch toward the note of water over water through the finger's-width door and **did not come down at once but stayed up through the whole of one of the breaths Sella counted at her fire in the room that does not touch this one --- the small intake, the hold, and on into the letting go --- and came down with the breath rather than before it, against the fold**, the longest holding yet, a coordination and not a reach, not a greater reach and not a stronger hand, filed on a place of its own beside the others and never laid across them, not a reaching, not a hearing, not a seventh, not a stopping, not a payment, not one of the three held changes, not a key, not a power and not a rule, with no payer named and no doctrine stood up out of it, while he still could not close the two inches to the pot handle, lift his chin to the crust or speak a number, the body humiliating him and moving by an inch at the same time, and one hundred and ninety-seven standing exact, complete and unspent in the dark of the wool, nothing joined, named, opened, called, spent or ended, the count going forward into Day 198, Ember 19 (T071/T072, T061/T082, T088).
Day 198 | Ember 19, Y.H. 813 (Ninthday) | under the Vail kiln-house roof at the bed, hearth, kiln mouth and loaf shelf's low angle, the Clay Yard, the yard well and the trough, the drying racks and the space beneath the lowest rack, the wall where the shallow pan stood, the field's empty place and the place the carriers crossed, the kindling, the flagstones, the wheel-shed edge, the clay road at the going of the light, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | The hand outside the wool went up an inch toward the note of water over water through the finger's-width door and came down before the counted breath had finished --- a shorter holding, the body keeping its own time, an inch let go early and no failure in it --- while the boy came into his hundred and ninety-eighth day, six months and eighteen days, and nobody under any roof said either number, the carried fire answering four low pulls with its fifth missing and its payer unnamed for a hundred and ninety-eight days and the river's fifth note and the boards' four nails, four and a thumb-width knot keeping three counts in three places that did not become one (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); hot clay came under tallow and wet wool under that with the sour corner breath, the crust gone leathery two inches from his ear with its empty place unnamed and no third, the drool thread frozen and unwiped, cold stopped at cheek, temple and the bare wrist joint, and the afterdrop waiting on a warming nobody would perform, while Sella Vail gave the instruction to the wool, the fire, the pot, the broom and the running water and never the basket, said the house, kept her half-pace, glanced once at the clay jar cold under its glaze without touching it, fed him gruel from the house's own dish with the spoon on her sleeve, pushed coal-black hair back with the burn-wrist so the grey streak showed pale, set the pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand with a half-beat at the setting, counted under her breath toward no opening and left the widow's mark untouched, for a morning and a shelf are not openings and there had been none since before the kiln on Ember 1 (T058/T079); a neighbour at the threshold with dry mud-scurf on her hem asked what the firing would want now the second reading stood a day off and was sent to the pot and the weather with nothing priced, refused or entered, and the sweet edge of lead glaze came up off High Kiln on a wrong wind and went out on the same wind as a direction answered with the pot and the weather, no sickness named in Grathic, no collector called and no lesson made (T077, T064 held); the yard went around the pan's gone round ring, the bare dark trough, the rack space with its broken birth-slates, the field's empty place and the place the carriers crossed with a look and a wide step and no number, the arm carrying him keeping the height of the handle, the give of the shoulder and the turn of the wrist at a step's end, a record in muscle that remembered weight and not cost (T009/T013/T092, T098); at the kindling Tarn took the broom only after it had been instructed, six counted in the worn thumb-mark and the seventh at the lip beside Garl's unforged collector sentence, brought the ear-sentence to the word that would call the loan and stopped, kept the Seed 16 breath unspent and the nine stoppings nine with no tenth before the seventh year was earned, his stroke going over the flagstones past the bright patch without the half-check and over the collector road without turning, no collector coming (T036/T076); Jorra kept dusty and clean forearms on one tray, parted dust from slip, told wheel and pot their sayings, withheld the note he knew three turns ahead from a man on a wrong wind, and held his bent finger in the cold damper until Sella's sentence reached the pot, a remembered repetition, with he's not Vail sealed and the promise ungiven (T035/T031/T062, T065/T095); in the small hours the file came back exact, complete and worth nothing, six hearings beside and never among with no seventh, three near-full answers advanced, the told bell beside the heard and the told figure beside the paid, three held changes on three places, the loft scrape on a fourth, the pale line on a fifth and the held inch on a sixth, the stone cold in its loft pot, the charcoal unread, the cost of the carried man entered nowhere, and one hundred and ninety-eight standing exact in the wool while the market stood a day off and did not get to read the inch (T061/T082, T006, T064, T060/T086/T097, T100, T004).
Day 199 | Ember 20, Y.H. 813 (Tenthday, the second Account-day and market-day of Ember) | the Cross, the reading block, the moot-post's finger's width of shade, the pot board and stalls, the out-of-line landing below the north bridge, the clay road, the Vail kiln-house roof at the hearth, loaf shelf, kindling and bed, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | The kiln month's second Account-day and market came up over the Cross with the boy one hundred and ninety-nine days old and six months and nineteen days and nobody saying so, Tallow still the firing month and Ember the kiln month and neither corrected, the carried fire's payer unnamed for a hundred and ninety-nine days and river, fire and boards keeping three counts in three places that did not become one (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); Sella Vail set the basket in the moot-post's finger's width of shade where no slate lay, stood half a pace off, gave the instruction to the wool and the river wind and never the basket, kept her hand from the mark because a reading was not an opening, and did not look into the basket (T058/T079); two hundred leaned to the reading block and eight pigs went over the wet boards while the bell went four, four and a gap with nobody crossing himself, the heard kept beside the told (T078); **the chapter's one meaningful change:** Berr Clay came up short off the clay road with the red wool cord of his office folded square under his coat and the green of tasted lead glaze at one mouth-corner, wiped his thumb twice before touching nothing, held a narrow slate unopened to his forearm, passed a finger over the figure, read One in twelve, as the notice says low into the top of the crowd --- the figure going twice over the basket, once for the moot and once for the households that came late --- said The levy is called. It is not called by me and your house, never I, and went off toward High Kiln with the box locked and the true subtraction inside, believing he had done a kindness, so that the district's arithmetic for the kiln month changed for the second time and could not be changed back, the printed figure standing unchallenged and a figure read aloud still not the figure paid (T015/T041/T047/T077); the reading did not answer the man carried out of High Kiln breathing while the month was young --- no figure, collector or slate knew what he cost, no cause was named in Grathic and his pale line stayed on its own place, a breath kept not an ending and no offstage fate invented (T064 held); Hessa Rale carried the slate up the road before noon edge-on to her forearm with the writing to her sleeve, telling the women who asked I don't read it. I carry it. There's a difference, and the road doesn't care which and Cross to Greybank. Reading goes up before noon.
