I've got a road to walk, her hand to the slate before her eyes to the yard, the eighth record-first hand joined to none and no figure coming off the slate (T080); below the north bridge the soft-cuffed clerk stayed set at the out-of-line landing for a measure nobody called, cuff grey from washing and grey again by midday, hand to slate before eyes to stalls and a buyer's number entered before he looked up to find the man who had said it, the market selling over him as water over a stone (T090); across the square a silent woman with clay under her nails, a pale line across the back of one hand and green in the crease beside her lip turned a cup a quarter and went off with the light, the sixth green a colour joined to no ear and never Fenna in narration (T050/T051 held), and at the market's edge a small shape in cast-offs with silt dried pale in its wrist creases, a gap where a front tooth had been and a green sliver on a string at its throat stopped its half-beat toward a pot and rubbed the sliver, unnamed, silent, no loaf, slate or sale and not a seventh sighting (T033/T034 held); Berr's twice-wiped thumb, Hessa's hand to the slate and the clerk's hand to his slate lay on places of their own, two inward records still two inward records, and a row of such hands would call itself an account (T080/T090); carried home at the going of the light with woodsmoke before and river wind behind, Sella went wide around the pan's gone ring, the bare trough, the rack space, the field's empty place and the place the carriers crossed, a neighbour's cost-question going to the pot and the weather and away level (T009/T013/T092, T098, T077); under the roof the figure had not crossed the wall, and in the small hours the hand outside the wool went up an inch and came down at once with no holding in it, the asking not always answered, the miss filed on the sixth place beside the holdings, not a hearing, seventh, stopping, payment, key, power or rule, with one hundred and ninety-nine exact and unspent in the wool and the market's figure not entered anywhere under the roof (T061/T082).
Day 200 | Ember 21, Y.H. 813 (Firstday) | under the Vail kiln-house roof at the bed, hearth, kiln mouth, loaf shelf's low angle and kindling, the Clay Yard, the yard well and the trough, the drying racks and the space beneath the lowest rack, the wall where the shallow pan stood, the field's empty place, the place the carriers crossed, the wheel-shed edge and its finger's-width-open back room (not entered), the cold damper, the flagstones, the Debt Loft (heard only), the clay road at the going of the light, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | The kiln month's last working week opened as an ordinary morning with no boundary drawn, the boy two hundred days old and six months and twenty days and nobody saying so, the carried fire's payer unnamed for two hundred days and the river's fifth note, the kiln's four-and-a-gap and the boards' four nails, four and a thumb-width knot keeping three counts in three places that did not become one (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); hot clay came under tallow and wet wool with the sour corner breath, the crust leathery two inches from his ear, the thread frozen and unwiped, cold stopped at cheek, temple and the bare wrist joint, and Sella Vail gave the instruction to the wool, fire, pot, broom, running water and river wind and never the basket, fed him from the house's own dish with the spoon on her sleeve, pushed coal-black hair back with the burn-wrist so the grey streak showed pale, set the pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand with a half-beat at the setting, counted under her breath toward no opening, kept her finger from beneath the shelf so the charcoal stayed unread and left the widow's mark untouched, for a morning and a shelf are not openings (T058/T079, T100); at the well a rack woman with slip dried pale in her wrists asked what the pots would want before the kiln month was out and a man come off the clay road asked what the kiln would want now the figure had gone up twice, and both were sent to the pot, the house and the weather with nothing priced, refused or entered, while the sweet edge of lead glaze came up off High Kiln on a wrong wind and went out on the same wind as a direction, no sickness named in Grathic, no collector called and no second edge paid so soon after the carrying-out (T077, T064 held); the yard went around the clean dark line and the finer grey called the thaw, the birth-slates beneath the lowest rack, the pan's gone round ring, the bare dark trough, the field's empty place and the place the carriers crossed with a look and a wide step and no number, no third gap and no fourth likeness made a cause and no finger of his drawing the line (T009/T013/T092, T098, T060/T086/T097); before first grey three or four unnamed men went low into the back room and got no name, the two shut-room tellings staying two (T035/T031, T065/T095); Jorra parted dust from slip as a surgeon parts clot from vessel, kept dusty and clean forearms on one tray, told wheel and pot their sayings, withheld the note known three turns ahead from a man on a wrong wind whose saying stayed a saying about a pot, and held his bent finger in the cold damper until Sella's sentence reached the pot, a remembered repetition not a change made again, with he's not Vail sealed and the promise ungiven (T035/T031/T062), while Tarn took the broom only after instruction, told the kindling Six counted. The next I don't count, brought the ear-sentence to the word that would call the loan and stopped, kept the nine stoppings nine with no tenth before the seventh year was earned, the Seed 16 breath unspent and the loan uncalled, his stroke over the flagstones past the bright patch without the half-check and over the collector road without turning, no collector coming (T036/T076); in the morning the hand outside the wool went up an inch and held for the length of the small intake only, coming down before the letting go, and in the small hours it went up again and stayed through the whole of one of the breaths Sella counted at her fire in the room that does not touch his, through the small intake and the hold and on into the letting go, coming down with the breath rather than before it --- a coordination with a rhythm he could not enter, not a greater reach, not a stronger hand and not a power, filed on its sixth place beside the others and never laid across them, with no payer named (T061/T082); the file came back exact, complete and worth nothing, six hearings with no seventh, three near-full answers, the told bell beside the heard and the told figure beside the paid, three held changes on three places, the loft scrape on a fourth, the pale line on a fifth and the held inch on a sixth, the stone cold in its loft pot, the charcoal unread, the cost of the carried man entered nowhere, and two hundred standing exact and unspent in the wool while the river, the fire and the boards kept three counts in three places that did not become one and the count went forward into Day 201 (T006, T064, T100, T004).
Day 201 | Ember 22, Y.H. 813 (Secondday) | under the Vail kiln-house roof at the bed, hearth and loaf shelf's low angle, the Clay Yard, the yard well, the drying racks, the kindling, the flagstones, the wheel-shed edge and its finger's-width-open back room (not entered), the Debt Loft (heard only), the clay road at the going of the light, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | The kiln month's last tend opened as an ordinary morning with no boundary drawn under anything, the boy two hundred and one days old and six months and twenty-one days and nobody under any roof saying so, the first smell hot clay under tallow and wet wool with the sour Ash breath of the unswept corner and the first sound the kiln drawing in fours with a gap rather than a bell, the carried fire missing its fifth with its payer unnamed for two hundred and one days while the river's fifth low note and the boards' four nails, four and a thumb-width knot kept three counts in three places that did not become one (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); the one leathery crust lay two inches from his ear with its empty place unnamed and no third, the drool thread frozen and unwiped, cold stopped at cheek, temple and the bare wrist joint with the afterdrop waiting on a warming nobody would perform, while Sella Vail gave the instruction to the wool, fire, pot, broom, running water and river wind and never the basket, said the house, kept her half-pace, glanced once at the clay jar without touching it, fed him from the house's own dish with the spoon on her sleeve, set the pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand with a half-beat at the setting, moved her lips along a two-gapped row without a figure, kept her finger from beneath the charcoal and left the widow's mark untouched, for a morning and a shelf are not openings and there had been none since before the kiln on Ember 1 (T058/T079, T100); a neighbour at the threshold with dry mud-scurf on her hem asked what the firing would want now the last tend was in and was told to ask the pot and the weather, going away level with nothing priced, refused or entered (T077), while three or four men went low into the back room before first grey and got no name and the two tellings of the shut counting room stayed two with no third (T035/T031/T065/T095); then the hand outside the wool went up an inch toward the note of water over water and came down at once in the morning, and in the small hours went up and held for the length of the small intake only, coming down before the letting go, each answer filed on its sixth place beside the others as not a hearing, seventh, stopping, payment, change, key, power or rule with no payer named, and two hundred and one stood exact in the wool, complete and unspent, spoken by nobody, as the count went forward into Day 202 (T061/T082).
Day 202 | Ember 23, Y.H. 813 (Thirdday) | under the Vail kiln-house roof at the bed, hearth and loaf shelf's low angle, the Clay Yard, the yard well, the trough, the drying racks, the kindling, the flagstones, the wheel-shed edge, the back room (not entered) and the Debt Loft (heard only), the clay road at the going of the light, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | The last working week went on into its third day without a boundary drawn, the boy two hundred and two days old and six months and twenty-two days and nobody saying so, the carried fire's payer unnamed for two hundred and two days and the fire, river and boards keeping three counts that did not become one (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); the sweet edge of lead glaze came up off High Kiln on a wrong wind, touched the fold and went out on the same wind, read as a direction and entered nowhere, and the saying that a pot singing before its time generally sings itself out early stayed a saying about a pot and was not made true as a lesson (T064); Sella Vail gave the instruction to the wool, fire, pot, broom, running water and river wind and never the basket, said the house, kept her half-pace, glanced once at the clay jar without a touch, fed him from the house's own dish with the spoon on her sleeve and his mouth unwiped, set the pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand with a half-beat at the setting, kept her finger from beneath the charcoal and left the widow's mark untouched because a morning and a shelf are not openings (T058/T079, T100); at the yard well a rack woman with slip dried pale in the creases of her wrists asked what the pots would want before the kiln month was out and a man come off the clay road with a number shut in his fist asked what the kiln would want now the figure had gone up twice, and both were sent to the pot, the house and the weather, going away level with nothing priced, refused or entered while the number in the fist arrived nowhere (T077); the second reading of Ember stood offstage and unchallenged, the box gone up the locked road toward High Kiln with the true subtraction inside, the printed one in twelve still printed, a figure gone twice over the basket still not the figure paid, Hessa's slate unread at Greybank with the writing to her sleeve and the soft-cuffed clerk still set at the out-of-line landing for a measure nobody called (T015/T041/T047, T080, T090 held); Tarn told the kindling six counted and the next he did not count with the knife at the sill's edge and Garl's unforged sentence beside it, brought the ear-sentence all the way to the word that would call the loan and stopped with no figure and no name, kept the Seed 16 breath unspent and nine stoppings with no tenth before the seventh year was earned, nearer its end than ever and not given, and swept the collector's road without turning while no collector came (T036/T076), and Jorra parted dust from slip, withheld a note known three turns ahead from a man on a wrong wind whose saying cost no pot, and held his bent finger in the cold damper until the sentence reached the pot, remembered and not made again (T035/T031, T061/T082, T062); then the hand went up an inch and came down before the counted breath had finished in the morning, an inch let go early and no failure in it, and in the small hours went up, stayed through the small intake, the hold and the letting go of one of the breaths Sella counted at her fire in the room that did not touch his, and came down with the breath --- the old coordination remembered, not a change made again --- each answer filed on its sixth place and laid across nothing, and two hundred and two stood exact in the wool, complete and unspent, spoken by nobody (T061/T082).
Day 203 | Ember 24, Y.H. 813 (Fourthday) | under the Vail kiln-house roof at the bed, hearth, loaf shelf's low angle and kindling, the Clay Yard, the yard well and the trough, the drying racks, the flagstones, the wheel-shed edge, the finger's-width-open back room (not entered), the cold damper, the Debt Loft (heard only), the clay road at the going of the light, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | The last tend ran on into its third working day with no boundary drawn and the seventh year standing nearer its end than ever at the lip of the knife's sill with six counted where the house could check and the seventh where it could not, the boy two hundred and three days old and six months and twenty-three days and nobody saying so, the carried fire's payer unnamed for two hundred and three days and the fire, river and boards keeping three counts that did not become one (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082, T036/T076); Sella Vail gave the instruction where it always went, to the wool, fire, pot, broom, running water and river wind and never the basket, said the house, kept her half-pace, glanced once at the clay jar without touching it, fed him from the house's own dish with the spoon on her sleeve and his mouth unwiped, set the pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand with a half-beat at the setting, kept her finger from beneath the charcoal and left the widow's mark untouched, for a morning and an afternoon are not openings and there had been none since before the kiln on Ember 1 (T058/T079, T100); a cost-question at the door went to the pot and the weather and away level with nothing entered (T077), neighbours wanting the thaw early and the late one were each answered Yes, house by Tarn, who went on wanting the late because it puts off the paying though the paying had stood in the ruts since the water went, brought the ear-sentence to the word that would call the loan and stopped with no figure and no name, kept the Seed 16 breath unspent and nine stoppings with no tenth, and swept the collector's road without turning while no collector came (T036/T076); three or four men went low into the back room before first grey and got no name, the two shut-room tellings staying two (T035/T031/T065/T095), and Jorra came through with fine grey slate dust ground small as meal past the bent finger, kept dusty and clean forearms on one tray, told a pot You'll do, withheld its full note from a man on a wrong wind whose saying stayed a saying and whose pot was not lost, and held his bent finger in the cold damper until the sentence reached the pot, remembered and not made again (T061/T082, T062); in the morning the hand went up an inch and held for the length of the small intake only, coming down before the letting go, but in the small hours it went up and stayed through the small intake, the hold and the letting go of one of the breaths Sella counted at her fire in the room that did not touch his and **did not come down with her breath**, keeping the small height through the little silence after her count had ended and coming down on the river's next low note --- a coordination with a second rhythm nobody counted and that was never given to him, not a longer reach, not a stronger hand, not a hearing since water over water remained a fifth low thing never counted among the hearings, not a seventh, stopping, payment, one of the three held changes, key, power or rule, with no payer named and nothing stood up out of it to call itself a sign (T061/T082, advanced and not paid); he still could not close the two inches to the pot handle, lift the crust two inches to his mouth or speak a number, the file stayed exact, complete and worth nothing, and two hundred and three stood exact in the dark, complete, unspent and spoken by nobody while the river went on without noticing that an inch had ridden one of its low notes and the count went forward into Day 204 (T006, T064, T100, T004).
Day 204 | Ember 25, Y.H. 813 (Fifthday) | under the Vail kiln-house roof at the bed, hearth and loaf shelf's low angle, the kindling, the Clay Yard, the yard well, the drying racks, the wheel-shed edge, the finger's-width-open back room (not entered), the cold damper, the Debt Loft (heard only), the clay road at the going of the light, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | The kiln month's last working week opened as an ordinary morning with no boundary drawn under the tend that had turned four days back, the boy two hundred and four days old and six months and twenty-four days and nobody under any roof saying so, the first smell hot clay under tallow and wet wool under that with the sour Ash breath of the unswept corner called the thaw and the first sound the kiln drawing in fours with a gap rather than a bell, the carried fire's payer unnamed for two hundred and four days while the river's fifth note of water over water and the boards' four nails, four and a thumb-width knot kept three counts in three places that did not become one, for agreeing them would have wanted a payer and none stood to any (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); one leathery brown-edged crust lay two inches from his ear with its empty place still unnamed and no third, the drool thread frozen and unwiped, cold stopped at cheek, temple and the bare wrist-joint with the afterdrop waiting on a warming nobody would perform, and the file turned out before the grey as instruments on a towel was exact, complete and worth nothing --- six hearings beside and never among with the sixth from a person and no seventh, three near-full answers advanced and unpaid, the told bell beside the heard and the told figure beside the paid, nine stoppings with no tenth before the seventh year was earned, three held changes on three places with the loft scrape on a fourth, the pale line on a fifth and the held inch on a sixth, the crust's gap, the shelf's two gaps, the pan's gone ring and the field's empty place on trays that did not touch (T060/T086/T097, T100, T006); the household kept Garl in objects and not in a story --- the salt coat holding a broader man's shape on its peg, the seat keeping its twenty-year leftward hollow, the clean dark line and the finer grey called the thaw unswept, the proverb gone to the pot and the debt against a firing of tiles circled without a name, neither paid nor settled nor stopped nor called, no collector coming up the clay road (T009/T013/T092); Sella Vail gave the instruction to the wool, fire, pot, broom, running water and river wind and never the basket, said the house, kept her half-pace, glanced once at the clay jar and shut her hand half over nothing without a touch, pushed coal-black hair back with the burn-wrist so the grey streak showed pale, fed him from the house's own dish with the spoon on her sleeve and his mouth unwiped, moved her lips along a two-gapped row without a figure, kept her finger from beneath the charcoal, told the fire it was the thaw, counted under her breath toward no opening and left the widow's mark where it had hung since the first firing, touched once before the kiln on Ember 1 and at no hour since (T058/T079, T100); a neighbour's cost-question about the firing went to the pot and the weather and away level with nothing priced, refused or entered (T077); at the kindling Tarn took the broom only after instruction, told the wood Six counted. The next I don't count with six in the worn thumb-mark and the seventh at the lip beside Garl's unforged collector sentence, brought the ear-sentence to the word that would call the loan and stopped with no figure and no name, drew the Seed 16 breath and did not spend it, kept nine stoppings with no tenth, his stroke over the flagstones without the half-check and over the collector road without turning while no collector came (T036/T076); three or four unnamed men went low into the back room before first grey and got no name, the two shut-room tellings staying two, and Jorra came through with meal-fine grey slate dust past the bent finger, not bone-coloured slip, parted dust from slip as clot from vessel, told the wheel it cared only for steady hands and a pot it would do, withheld the note he knew three turns ahead from a man on a wrong wind whose saying about a pot singing early stayed a saying and cost no pot, held his bent finger in the cold damper until the sentence reached the pot as a remembered repetition, and kept he's not Vail sealed with the promise of the boy's name ungiven (T035/T031/T062, T064, T065/T095); asked in the morning the hand outside the wool went up an inch toward the river's note and came down at once, the asking not always answered and the miss no failure, and in the small hours it went up and held for the small intake only, coming down before the letting go, each answer filed on the sixth place beside the others as not a hearing, seventh, stopping, payment, change, key, power or rule with no payer named, while he still could not close two inches, lift the crust or speak a number, and two hundred and four stood exact, complete, unspent and spoken by nobody as the count went forward into Day 205 (T061/T082).
Day 205 | Ember 26, Y.H. 813 (Sixthday) | under the Vail kiln-house roof at the bed, hearth, loaf shelf's low angle and kindling, the Clay Yard, the yard well and the bare trough, the drying racks, the wheel-shed edge, the finger's-width-open back room (not entered), the cold damper, the Debt Loft (heard only), the clay road at the going of the light, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | The word of the burned slate was the day's one change and it came up from Slate Row before the grey, spoken over the boards of the yard and not to the basket, a voice on the river wind saying that a slate had burned in the night and then going on to the price of barley and the weather, with no owner named, no room, no hand at the flame and no reason, and nobody in the yard turning it into a cause; the boy took it apart as a surgeon takes apart a wound and set it on a place of its own beside the dust off Jorra's forearms and the two tellings of the shut counting room, with a hand between the three, a direction arriving from a place, passing over boards and arriving nowhere, not a hearing, seventh, stopping, payment, fourth likeness or third telling and not carried into any chart a clerk would keep, advanced and not paid (T035/T031, T065/T095); the boy came into his two hundred and fifth day and six months and twenty-five days with nobody saying so, the fire's fifth without a payer for two hundred and five days and the river's fifth note, the kiln's four-and-a-gap and the boards' four, four and a thumb-width knot keeping three counts in three places that did not become one (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); hot clay came under tallow and wet wool with the sour corner breath, the crust leathery two inches from his ear with its empty place unnamed and no third, the thread frozen and unwiped, cold stopped at cheek, temple and the bare wrist joint, and the afterdrop waiting, while Sella Vail came to the fire before the basket, gave the instruction to the wool, fire, pot, broom, running water and river wind and never to the child, said the house, kept her half-pace, glanced once at the clay jar without a touch, pushed the coal-black hair back with the burn-wrist so the grey streak showed pale, fed him from her own dish with the spoon on her sleeve and his mouth unwiped, set the pot handle two inches beyond the hand with a half-beat at the setting and no look toward the bed, moved her lips along a two-gapped row without a figure, kept her finger from beneath the charcoal, counted under her breath toward no opening and left the widow's mark untouched, for a word over the boards and a slate burning in another quarter were not openings and there had been none in twenty-five days (T058/T079, T100); Jorra came through with meal-fine grey dust under the bent finger, grew quieter by a degree when the word went around the boards so that the household straightened around the quiet without an order, gave the house no name for anything, parted dust from slip as clot from vessel, spoke to the wheel and a pot, withheld the full note known three turns ahead from a wrong wind whose saying stayed a saying and cost no pot, and held his bent finger in the cold damper until the sentence reached the pot, a remembered repetition, with the damper read as one iron with two bites and he's not Vail sealed and the promise ungiven (T035/T031/T062); the sweet edge of lead glaze came up off High Kiln on that wrong wind, touched the fold and went out on the same wind, a second direction on the same day answered as a direction and entered nowhere, no second edge paid, no pot lost and no lesson stood up out of a breath of air (T064); at the yard well a rack woman with slip dried pale in her wrists and a man off the clay road with a number shut in his fist asked what the pots and the kiln would want with the last tend running, and both were sent to the pot, the house and the weather, going away level with nothing priced, refused or entered, the man's number arriving nowhere, while the figure of the second reading stood offstage in Berr's locked box with the true subtraction inside, Hessa's slate unread at Greybank and the soft-cuffed clerk still set for his uncalled measure (T077, T015/T041/T047/T080/T090 held); three or four unnamed men went low into the back room before first grey and got no name, and Tarn kept six where the house could check and the seventh at the lip beside Garl's unforged sentence, brought the ear-sentence to the word that would call the loan and stopped, kept the Seed 16 breath unspent and nine stoppings with no tenth, and swept past the bright patch and the collector road without turning while no collector came (T036/T076); in the morning the hand went up an inch and held for the small intake only, coming down before the letting go, and in the small hours it stayed through the small intake, the hold and the letting go of a breath counted at Sella's fire in the room that does not touch his and came down with the breath --- the old coordination remembered, not a change made again --- each answer filed on its sixth place and laid across nothing, while he still could not close two inches, lift the crust or speak a number and two hundred and five stood exact, complete, unspent and spoken by nobody as the count went forward into Day 206 (T061/T082).
Day 206 | Ember 27, Y.H. 813 (Seventhday) | under the Vail kiln-house roof at the bed, hearth, loaf shelf's low angle and kindling, the Clay Yard, the yard well, the drying racks, the flagstones, the wheel-shed edge, the finger's-width-open back room (not entered), the cold damper, the Debt Loft (heard only), the clay road at the going of the light, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | The kiln month's last working week ran into its third day with no boundary drawn and neither of the district's two names for the months corrected, the boy two hundred and six days old and six months and twenty-six days and nobody saying so, the carried fire's fifth unpaid for two hundred and six days and the river's fifth note, the kiln's four-and-a-gap and the boards' four, four and a thumb-width knot keeping three counts in three places that did not become one (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); the household kept Garl at its closest yet to the end of the tend and kept him in objects --- the salt-stiff coat with its left-shoulder crease, the seat with its twenty-year hollow, the clean dark line and the finer grey called the thaw gone around with a look and a wide step and neither swept, the proverb gone to the pot and the tile debt circled without a name, worse for being uncalled because it could be neither paid nor settled nor stopped, no collector called and no lesson made of any of it (T009/T013/T092); hot clay came under tallow and wet wool with the sour corner breath, the leathery crust stiff two inches from his ear with its empty place unnamed and no third, the thread frozen and unwiped, cold stopped at cheek, temple and the bare wrist joint, the afterdrop waiting, and his wanting to be told had gone so quiet he could no longer hear it asking though it was there all the same; Sella Vail came to the fire before the basket, told it the grey was the thaw, turned the coals with a broken pot, stood with the back of the pot counting under her breath toward no opening, said The house will keep it where another woman would say I, pushed coal-black hair back with the burn-wrist so the grey streak showed pale, glanced once at the clay jar and shut her hand half over nothing, moved her lips along a two-gapped row without a figure, kept her finger from beneath the charcoal, fed him from the house's own dish with the spoon on her sleeve and his mouth unwiped, set the pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand with a half-beat at the setting and no look toward the bed, and left the widow's mark where it had hung since the first firing, touched once before the kiln on Ember 1 and at no hour since --- no opening in twenty-six days (T058/T079, T100); a neighbour with dry scurf on her hem and a pot against her hip asked what the last of the kiln month would want before the account was read and was sent to the pot and the weather, going away level with nothing priced, refused or entered (T077); the sweet edge of lead glaze came up off High Kiln on a wrong wind, touched the fold and went out on the same wind, answered as a direction and entered nowhere, and the saying about a pot singing early stayed a saying and cost no pot (T064); Jorra came through with meal-fine grey dust past the bent finger, parted dust from slip as clot from vessel, quieter since the burned-slate word, told the wheel and a pot their sayings, withheld the note from the wrong wind, held his bent finger in the cold damper until the sentence reached the pot as a remembered repetition, read the iron as one piece with two bites with no Grathic word for both, and kept he's not Vail sealed with the promise of the boy's name ungiven, while the burned-slate word still went around the boards touching everything and belonging to nothing, nobody saying whose slate or which room or why, and the boy keeping it on its own place beside the dust and the two tellings with a hand between the three (T035/T031/T062, T065/T095); three or four unnamed men went low into the back room before first grey and got no name, and Tarn kept six counted where the house could check and the seventh at the lip beside Garl's unforged sentence, brought the ear-sentence all the way to the word that would call the loan and stopped with no figure and no name, drew the Seed 16 breath and did not spend it, kept nine stoppings with no tenth, the seventh nearer its end than ever and not given, his stroke over the bright patch and the collector road without the half-check or a turn while no collector came (T036/T076); in the morning the hand went up an inch and came down before the counted breath had finished, an inch let go early and no failure in it, but in the small hours, with Sella gone to her own bed and no count at her fire in the room that does not touch his to hold against, it went up an inch toward the note of water over water through the finger's-width door and stayed up for the length of the river's own low interval, the note drawing out and slackening, and came down on the next low note when the river gave it --- a coordination with the one rhythm through the door, not willed against another count and not a gain in strength, filed on the sixth place beside the others as not a longer reach, not a stronger hand, not a hearing since water over water remained a fifth low thing never counted among the hearings, not a seventh, stopping, payment, one of the three held changes, key, power or rule, with no payer named and no sign stood up (T061/T082 advanced and not paid); the file turned out at the close was exact, complete and worth nothing, six hearings with the sixth from a person and no seventh, three near-full answers unpaid, the told bell beside the heard and the told figure beside the paid, the three held changes and the loft scrape and pale line, nine stoppings with no tenth, the absences on trays that did not touch, the carried man's cost entered nowhere and the sixth green a colour and the foundling a shape, and he still could not close two inches, lift the crust or speak a number while two hundred and six stood exact in the dark, complete, unspent and spoken by nobody and the river went on over water without noticing that an inch had ridden one of its low notes, the count going forward into Day 207 (T006, T064, T100, T004).
Day 207 | Ember 28, Y.H. 813 (Eighthday) | under the Vail kiln-house roof at the bed, hearth, loaf shelf's low angle and kindling, the Clay Yard, the yard well, the drying racks, the wheel-shed edge, the finger's-width-open back room (not entered), the cold damper, the Debt Loft (heard only), the clay road at the going of the light, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | The kiln month's last working week ran on into its eighth morning with no boundary drawn at the turning of the tend and neither month-name corrected, the boy two hundred and seven days old and six months and twenty-seven days and nobody under any roof saying so, the carried fire's fifth without a payer for two hundred and seven days while the river's note of water over water, the kiln's four-and-a-gap and the boards' four nails, four and a thumb-width knot kept three counts in three places that did not become one, for agreeing them would have wanted a payer and none stood to any (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); the leathery brown-edged crust lay two inches from his ear with its empty place unnamed and no third, the drool thread frozen and unwiped, cold stopped at cheek, temple and the bare wrist-joint with the afterdrop waiting on a warming nobody would perform, and the file turned out before the grey as instruments on a towel was exact, complete and worth nothing --- six hearings beside and never among with the sixth from a person and no seventh, three near-full answers advanced and unpaid, the told bell beside the heard and the told figure beside the paid, nine stoppings with no tenth before the seventh year was earned, three held changes on three places with the loft scrape on a fourth, the pale line on a fifth and the held inch on a sixth, and the burned-slate word on its own place beside Jorra's dust and the two shut-room tellings with a hand between the three (T061/T082, T100); Sella Vail came to the fire before the basket and gave the instruction to the wool, the fire, the pot, the broom, the running water and the river wind and never to the basket, said the house, kept the half-pace, moved her lips along a two-gapped row without a figure, glanced once at the clay jar and shut her hand half over nothing without a touch, kept her finger on top of the shelf's front edge so the charcoal stayed unread, told the fire it was the thaw, turned the coals with the back of a broken pot, said The house will keep it, counted under her breath toward no opening, pushed coal-black hair back with the burn-wrist so the grey streak showed pale, set the pot rim out two inches beyond the reaching hand with a half-beat at the setting and did not look toward the bed, leaving the widow's mark untouched with no opening in twenty-seven days (T058/T079); a neighbour's cost-question about the firing was sent to the pot and the weather and went away level with nothing priced, refused or entered (T077); at the kindling Tarn told the wood Six counted. The next I don't count, said Yes, house to an early thaw and a late while wanting the late because it puts off the paying, brought the ear-sentence to the word that would call the loan and stopped with no figure and no name, kept the Seed 16 breath unspent, nine stoppings standing with no tenth and the collector road unswept, no collector coming (T036/T076); Jorra Vail came through with meal-fine grey dust parted from slip, spoke to the wheel before anyone (It doesn't care whose hands. It cares if they're steady), told a pot You'll do, withheld its full note from a man on a wrong wind whose saying stayed a saying and cost no pot, and held his bent finger in the cold damper until Sella's sentence reached the pot, a remembered holding and not a change made again (T035/T031/T062); the burned-slate word went over the boards and arrived nowhere, and the hand outside the wool went up an inch toward the river's note and came down before the counted breath had finished, a miss filed on its sixth place with no payer named (T061/T082); Garl was kept in objects to the last working week's end --- the salt coat's broader shape, the seat's hollow, the unswept line and finer grey, the proverb gone to the pot and the tile debt circled without a name, no lesson made, no collector called and his name unspoken for two hundred and seven days (T009/T013/T092).
Day 208 | Ember 29, Y.H. 813 (Ninthday) | under the Vail kiln-house roof at the bed, hearth, loaf shelf's low angle and kindling, the Clay Yard, the yard well and the bare trough, the drying racks, the flagstones, the wheel-shed edge, the finger's-width-open back room (not entered), the cold damper, the Debt Loft (heard only), the clay road's edge, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | A second day of directions came over the boards with no boundary drawn, the boy two hundred and eight days old and six months and twenty-eight days and nobody saying so, the carried fire's fifth unpaid for two hundred and eight days and the river's fifth note, the kiln's four-and-a-gap and the boards' four, four and a thumb-width knot keeping three counts in three places that did not become one (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); the burned-slate word went around the boards a second time, spoken over them and not to the basket, with no owner, room, hand at the flame or reason and the voice going on to the price of barley and the weather, and the boy took it apart as a surgeon takes apart a wound and filed it again on its own place beside Jorra's dust and the two shut-room tellings with a hand between the three --- a second telling of a direction, still not a third telling of the shut counting room, not a cause and nothing a clerk could chart (T035/T031/T065/T095); Jorra Vail came through with meal-fine grey dust ground small as meal past the bent finger and white in the creases of a fist, parted dust from slip, spoke to the wheel (It doesn't care whose hands. It cares if they're steady), told a pot You'll do, knew its full note three turns before it left the wheel and withheld it from a man come up on a wrong wind whose saying that a pot singing before its time generally sings itself out early stayed a saying about a pot with no pot lost and no lesson made (T061/T082); the sweet edge of lead glaze came up off High Kiln on that wrong wind, touched the fold and went out on the wind that brought it, answered as a direction and entered nowhere with no second edge paid, no cause named in Grathic and no collector called (T064 held); at the yard well a rack woman with slip dried pale in her wrists asked what the pots would want before the last market and a man off the clay road kept his number shut in his fist and paid nothing, both sent to the pot and the weather so nothing was slopped, priced, refused or entered, while the printed one in twelve stood unchallenged and the true subtraction stayed out of reach in Berr Clay's locked box up the road, and the boy sorted the yard in ninety seconds with a competence intact and useless because there was no hand in the basket to carry any of it to a slate (T077, T015/T041/T047 held); Sella Vail gave the instruction to the wool, fire, pot, broom, running water and river wind and never to the basket, said the house, kept the half-pace from open slates, moved her lips along a two-gapped row without a figure, kept her finger from beneath the charcoal, glanced once at the clay jar and shut her hand half over nothing, told the fire it was the thaw, turned the coals with a broken pot, said The house will keep it, counted under her breath toward no opening, pushed coal-black hair back with the burn-wrist so the grey streak showed pale, set the pot rim out two inches beyond the reaching hand with a half-beat at the setting and did not look toward the bed, the widow's mark untouched with no opening in twenty-eight days (T058/T079, T100); at the kindling Tarn told the wood Six counted. The next I don't count, said Yes, house to an early thaw and a late while going on wanting the late because it puts off the paying, brought the ear-sentence to the word that would call the loan and stopped, kept nine stoppings with no tenth before the seventh year was earned, the Seed 16 breath unspent and the loan uncalled, and swept past the bright patch and the collector road without turning, no collector coming (T036/T076); three or four unnamed men went low into the back room before first grey and Jorra gave them no name; in the morning the hand went up an inch toward the river's note and came down at once before the small intake had finished, an honest miss, and in the small hours it stayed through the small intake, the hold and the letting go of one of the breaths Sella counted at her fire in the room that does not touch his and came down with the breath --- the old coordination remembered, not a change made again --- each answer filed on its sixth place with no payer named and nothing standing up to call itself a hearing, seventh, stopping, payment, key, power or rule (T061/T082).
Day 209 | Ember 30, Y.H. 813 (Tenthday, the third Account-day and last market of Ember) | the Vail kiln-house roof at the bed, hearth, loaf shelf's low angle and kindling, the Clay Yard, the drying racks, the yard well, the flagstones, the wheel-shed edge, the back room (not entered), the cold damper, the Debt Loft (heard only), the clay road, the Cross, the reading block and the moot-post's finger's width of shade, the pot board and stalls, the Fishstairs and out-of-line landing below the north bridge, High Kiln, Greybank, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | The kiln month's third and last Account-day and last market came up over the Cross with the boy two hundred and nine days old and six months and twenty-nine days and nobody under any roof saying so, no slate in Sarn with a place to enter it, the carried fire's fifth without a payer for two hundred and nine days and the river's fifth low note, the kiln's four-and-a-gap and the boards' four, four and a thumb-width knot keeping three counts in three places that did not become one (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); Sella Vail carried him down the clay road at the half-pace in the morning with woodsmoke before her and river wind behind, kept the half-pace from every open slate, gave the instruction to the wool and the river wind and never to the basket, and set him in the moot-post's finger's width of shade where no slate lay with the cold iron at his cheek, standing half a pace off and saying nothing of the square or the thane's low voice (T058/T079); two hundred leaned to the reading block while eight pigs went over the wet boards with a man and a slate following every pig, and the bell went four, four and a gap with nobody crossing himself, the heard bell kept beside the told from the Long Night (T078); the chapter's one true change: Berr Clay came up short off the clay road a little before the top of the morning with the red wool cord of his office folded square under his coat and the green of tasted lead glaze at one corner of his mouth, wiped his thumb twice before touching nothing, held a narrow slate unopened to his forearm, passed a finger over the figure and read One in twelve, as the notice says low into the top of the crowd --- the figure going twice over the basket, once for the moot and once for the households that come late --- said The levy is called. It is not called by me and your house, never I, taking himself out of his own sentence as a surgeon takes his hand out of a field before the dressing goes on, and went off toward High Kiln with the box locked and the true subtraction inside, believing he had done a kindness, so that the district's arithmetic changed for the third time in the kiln month where two hundred people stood and could not be changed back, the printed figure standing unchallenged and a figure read aloud still not the figure paid (T015/T041/T047/T077); Berr's twice-wiped thumb joined the record-first hands on the widened tray --- the midwife's reach for a slate before the child, the thane's finger over the figure, the clerk's number before the eyes found the man, the wheel-keeper's word to the wheel, Sella's finger on the shelf's front edge, Hessa's hand to her slate and the soft-cuffed clerk's inward reaching, eight hands going to the record before the person and held apart by hand because a row of eight standing long enough will call itself an account and an account wants a payer, and none stands; Hessa Rale took the slate up the road toward Greybank before noon edge-on to her forearm with the writing to her sleeve, her hand arriving before her eyes took the crowd, telling the women who asked what she carried I don't read it. I carry it. There's a difference, and the road doesn't care which and Cross to Greybank. Reading goes up before noon. I've got a road to walk, answering the question put and not the one behind it, a runner and not a reader with no figure coming off the slate, her reaching filed as the eighth record-first hand, advanced and not paid (T080); below the north bridge at the Fishstairs on the out-of-line landing where a fifth step should have stood the soft-cuffed clerk stayed set for a measure nobody called, cuff grey from washing and grey again by midday, hand to slate before eyes to stalls, nobody stepping around him or taking him off, the market selling over him as water goes over a stone (T090); the reading answered nothing for the glaze-room hand carried out on Day 188 --- no figure, collector, slate, name, cause or offstage death --- and the pale line stayed on its own place; at the pot board the sixth green stayed a colour, turned a cup a quarter and set it back, and at the market's edge the foundling stayed a shape with a green sliver going dull and a thumb rubbed to it, no seventh of either coming to Ember's last market and neither named as Fenna or Ulda in the narration (T050/T051, T033/T034 held); the crowd leaned and straightened and went back to its pots and pigs, and the red wool cord went up the clay road folded square under Berr's coat with the box locked and the true subtraction inside; at the going of the light Sella carried him home around the pan's gone ring, the bare dark trough, the broken birth-slates, the field's empty place and the place the carriers crossed, each with a look and a wide step and no number, and set him on his bed under the roof and not in the Debt Loft, fold right and thread frozen (T009/T013/T092/T098); in the small hours he turned out the file as instruments on a towel and found it exact, complete and worth nothing, six hearings beside and never among with the sixth from a person and no seventh, three near-full answers advanced and unpaid, the told bell beside the heard, the told figure beside the paid, nine stoppings with no tenth, three held changes on three places with the loft scrape on a fourth, the pale line on a fifth and the held inch on a sixth, the burned-slate word on its own place and no third gap, no fourth likeness made a cause and no finger drawn across them (T061/T082, T100, T060/T086/T097); then, with Sella gone to her bed and no breathing number anywhere in the house, under the very conditions that had given the one-rhythm holding on Day 206, the hand went up an inch toward the river's note and came down at once against the fold --- a miss, the asking not always answered and no failure in the miss --- filed on its sixth place with no payer named and nothing standing up to call itself a reach, hearing, seventh, stopping, payment, held change, key, power or rule (T061/T082); he could not close two inches to the pot handle, lift his chin two inches to the crust or speak a number anybody would believe, and Ember went out without a boundary drawn, two hundred and nine standing exact, complete, unspent and spoken by nobody, the fire's fifth unpaid for two hundred and nine days and the count standing ready to go forward into a day nobody under the roof had named.
Day 210 | Grain 1, Y.H. 813 (Firstday) | under the Vail kiln-house roof at the bed, hearth, loaf shelf's low angle and kindling, the Clay Yard, the drying racks, the yard well, the bare trough, the wheel-shed edge, the finger's-width-open back room (not entered), the cold damper, the Debt Loft (heard only), the flagstones, the clay road at the going of the light, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | Ember gave way to Grain in the dark without a boundary drawn and neither of the district's two month-names was corrected, the boy two hundred and ten days old and six months and thirty days with no slate in Sarn to enter it, the fire's fifth unpaid for two hundred and ten days while the river's fifth low note and the boards' four nails, four and a thumb-width knot kept three counts in three places that did not become one, for agreeing them would have wanted a payer and none stood to any (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); the file turned out before the grey as instruments on a towel was exact, complete and worth nothing --- six hearings beside and never among with the sixth from a person and no seventh, three near-full answers advanced and unpaid, the told bell beside the heard, the told figure beside the paid with Ember's third true subtraction gone up the clay road in Berr's locked box, nine stoppings with no tenth before the seventh year at its very end and still ungiven, three held changes on three places with the loft scrape on a fourth, the pale line on a fifth and the held inch on a sixth, the burned-slate word on its own place beside Jorra's dust and the two shut-room tellings with a hand between the three, and the crust's gap, the shelf's two gaps, the pan's gone ring and the field's empty place on trays that did not touch (T061/T082, T100); Sella Vail came to the fire before the basket, gave the instruction to the wool, fire, pot, broom, running water and river wind and never the basket, said the house, moved her lips along a two-gapped loaf row with no figure, kept her finger on top of the shelf's front edge so the charcoal stayed unread, glanced once at the clay jar cold under its glaze and shut her hand half over nothing without a touch, told the fire it was the thaw, turned the coals with a broken pot, said The house will keep it, counted under her breath toward no opening, pushed coal-black hair back with the burn-wrist so the grey streak showed pale, set the pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand with a half-beat at the setting and no look toward the bed, and left the widow's mark untouched with no opening in thirty days (T058/T079, T100); a neighbour's cost-question about the new month's firing went to the pot and the weather and away level with nothing priced, refused or entered (T077); Tarn told the wood Six counted. The next I don't count with the knife at the sill's edge and Garl's unforged collector sentence beside it, brought the ear-sentence to the word that would call the loan and stopped with no figure and no name, kept the Seed 16 breath unspent and nine stoppings with no tenth, swept past the bright patch without the half-check and over the collector's road without a turn, and no collector came up or went down the clay road (T036/T076); Jorra Vail came through with meal-fine grey slate dust ground small as meal, parted dust from slip as clot from vessel, kept dusty and clean forearms on one tray, spoke to the wheel and told a pot You'll do, withheld its full note from a man come up on a wrong wind whose saying about a pot singing early stayed a saying and cost no pot, named no man who went low into the back room before first grey, joined nothing to the two shut-room tellings, and held his bent finger in the cold damper until Sella's sentence reached the pot as a remembered repetition (T035/T031/T062/T065/T095); the household kept Garl in objects at the turning --- the salt coat, the hollow seat, the unswept line and finer grey called the thaw, the proverb gone to the pot and the tile debt circled without a name --- with no lesson, no collector and his name unspoken for two hundred and ten days (T009/T013/T092); asked in the morning the hand went up an inch toward the river's note and came down before the counted breath had finished, filed on the sixth place with no payer named and nothing standing up as a power, and in the small hours it held for the small intake only and came down before the letting go (T061/T082).
Day 211 | Grain 2, Y.H. 813 (Secondday) | under the Vail kiln-house roof at the bed, hearth, loaf shelf's low angle and kindling, the Clay Yard, the kiln mouth, the drying racks, the lowest rack and its broken birth-slates, the pan's gone round ring, the yard well, the bare trough, the flagstones, the wheel-shed edge, the finger's-width-open back room (not entered), the cold damper, the Debt Loft (heard only), the clay road at the going of the light, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | The firing thinned into the ordinary tending of a year begun without a boundary drawn, so gradual that a man come into the yard at first grey would have said the kiln month had never been a month but one long morning's work, the boy two hundred and eleven days old and six months and thirty-one days with no slate in Sarn, Sill or Grath to enter either number, the carried fire's fifth without a payer for two hundred and eleven days and the river, the boards and the fire keeping three counts that did not become one (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); the yard went around the clean dark line where Ash had turned the corner, the new finer hairline called the thaw, the space beneath the lowest rack with its broken birth-slates, the circle the shallow clay pan had left, the bare dark trough whose water was called weather, the field's empty place and the place the carriers crossed, each with a look and a wide step and no number, the house keeping its arithmetic in loaves, glances and the space where a thing had stood (T009/T013/T092, T097/T098 carried); at the yard well a rack woman with slip dried pale in the creases of her wrists asked what the new month's pots would want now the firing was done and was sent to the pot and the weather with nothing priced, refused or entered, while a man come off the clay road kept a number shut in his fist, went off up the road with his arithmetic still his own and paid nothing, two directions coming over the boards in one morning and neither landing (T077, T058/T079); Berr Clay's box stayed locked going toward High Kiln with Ember's third true subtraction inside, the printed one in twelve stood unchallenged, Hessa Rale's slate lay unread at Greybank with the writing to her sleeve, and the soft-cuffed clerk stayed set at the out-of-line landing below the north bridge for a measure nobody called, all held offstage in the file, none opened and none reaching back (T015/T041/T047, T080, T090 held); Sella Vail came to the fire before the basket, gave the instruction to the wool, fire, pot, broom, running water and river wind and never the basket, said the house, kept her half-pace and went wide around the pan's gone ring, moved her lips along a two-gapped row with no figure, kept her finger on top of the shelf's front edge, glanced once at the clay jar and shut her hand half over nothing, told the fire it was the thaw, turned the coals with a broken pot, said The house will keep it, counted under her breath toward no opening, pushed coal-black hair back with the burn-wrist so the grey streak showed pale, set the pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand with a half-beat and no look toward the bed, and left the widow's mark untouched because a morning shelf and a well are not openings, no opening in thirty-one days (T058/T079, T100); Tarn at the kindling told the wood Six counted. The next I don't count with the knife at the sill's lip, said Yes, house to an early thaw and a late while wanting the late because it puts off the paying, brought the ear-sentence to the word that would call the loan and stopped with no figure and no name, kept the Seed 16 breath unspent and nine stoppings with no tenth before the seventh year at its very end, swept over the flagstones past the bright patch without the half-check and over the collector's road without a turn, and no collector came (T036/T076); Jorra Vail came through with meal-fine grey slate dust, kept dusty and clean forearms on one tray, told a pot You'll do, withheld its knowing from a wrong-wind man whose saying stayed a saying, gave no name to the men who went low into the back room before first grey, kept the burned-slate word on its own place beside the dust and the two tellings with a hand between, and held his bent finger in the cold damper until Sella's sentence reached the pot (T035/T031/T062/T065/T095); Sella carried him home at the going of the light around the absences with a look and a wide step and no number, set him on his bed and not in the Debt Loft, put the fold right without a look at his face and left the thread frozen; asked in the morning the hand went up an inch toward the note and came down at once against the fold, an honest miss with no failure in it, and in the small hours it stayed for the river's own low interval, the draw, the slack and the small pause, and came down on the river's next low note, the one-rhythm coordination remembered and not a change made again, filed on the sixth place with no payer and nothing standing up as a power (T061/T082).
Day 212 | Grain 3, Y.H. 813 (Thirdday) | under the Vail kiln-house roof at the bed, hearth, loaf shelf's low angle and kindling, the Clay Yard, the kiln mouth, the drying racks, the lowest rack, the pan's gone round ring, the yard well, the bare trough, the flagstones, the wheel-shed edge, the finger's-width-open back room (not entered), the cold damper, the Debt Loft (heard only), the clay road at the going of the light, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | Grain's third day came on in the small hours as a smell and then a gap in a sound, its first tend running on without a boundary drawn and neither of the district's two month-names put right, with no market and no Account-day until Grain 10 nine mornings off, the boy two hundred and twelve days old and six months and thirty-two days with no slate in Sarn, Sill or Grath to enter either number, the carried fire's fifth without a payer for two hundred and twelve days and the river, the boards and the fire keeping three counts that did not become one (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); the yard went around the clean dark line, the thaw, the broken birth-slates beneath the lowest rack, the pan's gone round ring, the bare trough with its water called weather, the field's empty place and the place the carriers crossed, each with a look and a wide step and no number, and he took the empty places one at a time with no finger drawn across them, no third gap and no fourth likeness made a cause (T009/T013/T092, T097/T098); Sella Vail came to the fire before the basket, gave the instruction to the wool, fire, pot, broom, running water and river wind and never the basket, said the house, kept her half-pace and went wide around the pan's gone ring, moved her lips along a two-gapped row with no figure, kept her finger on top of the shelf's front edge so the charcoal stayed unread, glanced once at the clay jar and shut her hand half over nothing, told the fire it was the thaw, turned the coals with a broken pot, said The house will keep it, counted under her breath toward no opening, pushed coal-black hair back with the burn-wrist so the grey streak showed pale, set the pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand with a half-beat and no look toward the bed, and left the widow's mark untouched because a shelf and a morning are not openings, no opening in thirty-two days, her private roll unshown and Dessa's cost unentered (T058/T079, T100); a neighbour with dry scurf on her hem and a pot hitched against her hip asked what Grain's pots would want now the firing was done and was told Ask the pot, the house has said it, ask the weather too, going away level with nothing priced, refused or entered (T077); Tarn told the wood Six counted. The next I don't count with the knife at the sill's lip, brought the ear-sentence to the word that would call the loan and stopped, kept the Seed 16 breath unspent and nine stoppings with no tenth before the seventh year at its very end and still ungiven, swept past the bright patch without the half-check and over the collector's road without a turn, and no collector came in the third morning of Grain (T036/T076); Jorra Vail came through with meal-fine grey slate dust past the bent finger and under the nails, parted dust from slip as clot from vessel, kept dusty and clean forearms on one tray, told a pot You'll do, withheld its full note from a High Kiln man on a wrong wind whose saying about a pot singing early stayed a saying and cost no pot, gave no name to the men who went low into the back room before first grey, kept the burned-slate word on its own place beside the dust and the two tellings with a hand between, grew quieter, and held his bent finger in the cold damper until Sella's sentence reached the pot (T035/T031/T062/T065/T095); Garl was kept to the third day without a story, the salt coat holding a broader man's shape, the seat its hollow and leftward lean, the clean dark line and the finer grey unswept, the proverb gone to the pot and the tile debt circled without a name, his name unspoken for two hundred and twelve days (T009/T013/T092); Sella carried him home at the going of the light around the absences and set him on his bed, put the fold right without a look at his face and left the thread frozen; in the small hours he turned out the file as a man lays instruments on a towel and found it exact, complete and worth nothing, then asked his hand and had it go up an inch toward the note and hold for the small intake only and come down before the letting go in the morning, and in the small hours hold through the small intake, the hold and the letting go of one of the breaths Sella counts at her fire in the room that does not touch his, coming down with the breath --- the old two-rhythm coordination remembered, not a change made again --- filed on the sixth place with no payer named and nothing standing up as a power; he still could not close the two inches to the pot handle, lift his chin two inches to the crust or speak a number, and the count went forward into Day 213, Grain 4, Fourthday, with Grain 10 held as the first Account-day and market of Grain, where the seventh green and the seventh sighting are owed and must not come early, the green still a colour and the foundling a shape (T061/T082, T050/T051, T033/T034).
Day 213 | Grain 4, Y.H. 813 (Fourthday) | under the Vail kiln-house roof at the bed, hearth, loaf shelf's low angle and kindling, the Clay Yard, the drying racks, the lowest rack with its broken birth-slates, the pan's gone round ring, the yard well, the bare trough, the flagstones, the wheel-shed edge, the finger's-width-open back room (not entered), the cold damper, the Debt Loft (heard only), the clay road at the going of the light, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | the day came to the boy as hot clay under tallow and the kiln's four-and-a-gap with no bell while he lay two hundred and thirteen days old and six months and thirty-three days with no slate in Sarn to enter it, the file turned out as instruments on a towel exact, complete and worth nothing with six hearings beside and never among and no seventh, three near-full answers advanced and unpaid, the told bell beside the heard and the told figure beside the paid, nine stoppings and three held changes on three places with the loft scrape fourth, the pale line fifth and the held inch sixth, and the burned-slate word on its own place beside Jorra's dust and the two shut-room tellings with a hand between the three (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); the morning hand went up an inch toward the river's note and came down at once against the fold, an honest miss with no failure hidden under it and no payer named, while Sella came to the fire before the basket, told it the thaw, moved her lips along a two-gapped loaf row with no figure, kept her finger on top of the shelf's front edge, glanced once at the cold clay jar and shut her hand half over nothing, set the pot rim out with its handle two inches beyond the reaching hand and rested her hand a half-beat longer at the setting without looking toward the bed, and left the widow's mark untouched with no opening in thirty-three days (T058/T079, T100); a neighbour's cost-question at the threshold went to the pot and the weather and away level, the yard went around the clean dark line, the thaw, the broken birth-slates, the pan's gone ring, the bare trough, the field's empty place and the carriers' place each with a look and a wide step and no number, Tarn took the broom only after it had been instructed and told the wood Six counted. The next I don't count with the ear-sentence stopped at the word that would call the loan and no collector on Grain 4, and Jorra parted dust from slip at the wheel-shed, held his bent finger in the cold damper until Sella's sentence reached the pot and kept he's not Vail sealed (T036/T076, T035/T031/T065/T095); in the small hours the hand held for the small intake only and came down before the letting go, filed on the sixth place, and two hundred and thirteen stood exact, unspent and spoken by nobody into Grain 5.
Day 214 | Grain 5, Y.H. 813 (Fifthday) | under the Vail kiln-house roof at the bed, hearth, loaf shelf's low angle and kindling, the Clay Yard and its edge, the drying racks and the broken birth-slates beneath the lowest rack, the yard well, the bare trough, the flagstones, the wheel-shed edge, the finger's-width-open back room (not entered), the cold damper, the Debt Loft (heard only), the clay road, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | the boy came into his two hundred and fourteenth day and six months and thirty-four days with nobody saying so, the carried fire's fifth unpaid for two hundred and fourteen days and the fire, river and boards keeping three counts that did not become one (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); the chapter's one meaningful change: a kiln-hand come up from High Kiln on a wrong wind told the yard that the pot which sang before its time last month had not been heard from since, and the boy took the saying apart as a surgeon takes apart a wound --- a direction with no hand at the foot of it, no owner, no room, no cause and no figure --- and set it on the near-full tray beside the hearings and never among them, for a report of a silence is not a second hearing of the same pot, while Jorra, who knew the note that pot would give three turns before it left his hands, let the saying stand as a saying, gave no name to what ailed the man carried out on Day 188 and lost no pot (T064, T061/T082, T035/T031); the sweet edge of lead glaze came up off High Kiln on the wrong wind, touched the fold and went out on the wind that brought it as a direction and not a second edge paid, and at the yard well a rack woman with slip dried pale in her wrists and a man off the clay road with a number shut in his fist went to the pot and the weather with nothing priced, refused or entered while the printed one in twelve stood unchallenged and the true subtraction stayed locked in Berr's box answering nothing for the carried man (T077, T015/T041/T047 held); Sella kept her back to the basket and her finger above the charcoal, set the pot handle two inches beyond the hand with the half-beat and no look toward the bed, and left the mark untouched with no opening in thirty-four days (T058/T079, T100), Tarn brought the ear-sentence to the word that would call the loan as an interval and not a calling with nine stoppings and the Seed 16 breath unspent and no collector on Grain 5 (T036/T076), and in the small hours the hand kept the river's own low interval, the draw, the slack and the small pause, and came down on the next low note, a one-rhythm coordination remembered with no count anywhere in the house to hold it against (T061/T082).
Day 215 | Grain 6, Y.H. 813 (Sixthday) | under the Vail kiln-house roof at the bed, hearth, loaf shelf's low angle and kindling, the Clay Yard, the drying racks and the broken birth-slates beneath the lowest rack, the pan's gone round ring, the yard well, the bare trough, the flagstones, the wheel-shed edge, the finger's-width-open back room (not entered), the cold damper, the Debt Loft (heard only), the clay road at the going of the light, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | the burned-slate word came over the boards a third time, from Slate Row as a direction and not a visit, spoken on the same breath as the price of barley and the weather with no owner, room, hand at the flame or reason, and the boy took it apart as a surgeon takes apart a wound and set it on its own place beside Jorra's dust and the two shut-room tellings with a hand between the three --- a third telling of the burned slate and still not a third telling of the counting room shut in the war, advanced and not paid (T035/T031/T065/T095); Jorra grew quieter whenever it went around and the household straightened around the quiet without an order, the quiet going before him into the shed the way a cold air goes before a door, while he kept his dusty forearms apart from his clean ones, told a pot You'll do, withheld its full note, gave no name to what ailed the man carried out on Day 188 and sealed he's not Vail with the promise of the boy's name neither given nor withdrawn; two cost-questions, a neighbour's at the door about the mid-tend work and a rack woman's at the well about the pots' cost, went to the pot and the weather and away level with nothing priced, refused or entered (T077, T058/T079), and Sella kept her two-gapped loaf row, her finger above the charcoal and the clay jar untouched, set the pot handle two inches beyond the hand and left the widow's mark with no opening in thirty-five days (T100); Tarn's ear-sentence stopped at the word that would call the loan as a remembered stopping with nine stoppings, the Seed 16 breath unspent and no collector on Grain 6 (T036/T076), and in the morning the hand went up an inch under conditions that had sometimes answered and came down at once --- a miss --- while in the small hours it went up, stayed through the small intake and the hold of one of the breaths Sella counts at her fire and came down on the letting go, a coordination shorter than the whole breath of the night before, filed on the sixth place with nothing laid across it and no payer named (T061/T082); two hundred and fifteen stood exact, unspent and spoken by nobody into Day 216, Grain 7, with Grain 10 four mornings off and the seventh green still a colour and the foundling a shape (T050/T051, T033/T034).
Day 216 | Grain 7, Y.H. 813 (Seventhday) | under the Vail kiln-house roof at the bed, hearth, loaf shelf's low angle and kindling, the Clay Yard, the drying racks, the lowest rack with its broken birth-slates, the pan's gone round ring, the yard well, the bare trough, the flagstones, the wheel-shed edge, the finger's-width-open back room (not entered), the cold damper, the Debt Loft (heard only), the clay road, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | the second working week opened without a boundary drawn, the boy two hundred and sixteen days old and six months and thirty-six days and nobody saying so, the carried fire's fifth unpaid for two hundred and sixteen days while the river's note of water over water, the kiln's four-and-a-gap and the boards' four, four and a thumb-width knot kept three counts in three places that did not become one, for agreeing them would have wanted a payer and none stood to any (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); the file turned out as instruments on a towel was exact, complete and worth nothing, six hearings beside and never among with the sixth from a person and no seventh, three near-full answers advanced and unpaid now holding two sayings about the pot that sang early and is not heard from, nine stoppings with no tenth before the seventh year at its very end and still ungiven, three held changes on three places with the loft scrape fourth, the pale line fifth and the held inch sixth, and the burned-slate word told three times on its own place beside Jorra's dust and the two shut-room tellings with a hand between the three (T061/T082, T036/T076, T035/T031/T065/T095); word went up the clay road that stalls were being set at the Cross against Grain 10, a neighbour going up with boards on a barrow, and the boy took it as a direction with a date, for no market, Account-day, loaf, slate or sale came early into a working day (T077); Sella Vail came to the fire before the basket, gave every instruction to the wool, fire, pot, broom, running water and river wind and never the basket, said the house, moved her lips along a two-gapped row without a figure, glanced once at the clay jar cold under its glaze and shut her hand half over nothing, kept her finger on top of the shelf's front edge so the charcoal stayed unread, told the fire it was the thaw, turned the coals with a broken pot, said The house will keep it, counted under her breath toward no opening, set the pot rim out with its handle two inches beyond the reaching hand and rested a half-beat longer at the setting without looking toward the bed, left the widow's mark untouched with no opening in thirty-six days, and sent a neighbour's cost-question about the month's firing to the pot and the weather with nothing priced, refused or entered (T058/T079, T100, T077); Garl Vail was kept in objects and not in a story --- the salt-stiff coat with its left-shoulder crease, the seat's twenty-year hollow and leftward lean, the clean dark line and finer grey called the thaw unswept, the proverb gone to a pot with no crack and the tile debt circled without a name, worse for being uncalled --- no lesson made, no collector called and his name unspoken for two hundred and sixteen days (T009/T013/T092); the yard went around the broken birth-slates, the pan's gone round ring, the bare trough with its water called weather, the field's empty place and the carriers' place each with a look and a wide step and no number (T098); Tarn took the broom only after it had been instructed, told the wood Six counted. The next I don't count with the knife at the very edge and the seventh at the lip beside Garl's unforged collector sentence, brought the ear-sentence to the word that would call the loan and stopped with no figure and no name, kept the Seed 16 breath unspent, nine stoppings and the collector road without a turn, no collector coming on Grain 7 (T036/T076); Jorra Vail parted meal-fine dust from slip as clot from vessel, spoke to the wheel and told a pot You'll do, withheld the note known three turns ahead, gave no name to the men who went low into the back room before first grey, kept the two shut-room tellings at two, and held his bent finger in the cold damper until Sella's unfinished sentence reached the pot as a remembered repetition, with he's not Vail sealed and the promise ungiven (T035/T031/T062); Sella carried him home at the going of the light at the half-pace around the absences and set him on his bed, not in the loft, fold right and thread frozen; in the morning the hand went up an inch and came down at once against the fold, and in the small hours it held for the small intake only and came down before the letting go, an honest fraction filed on its sixth place with nothing laid across it and no payer named, and two hundred and sixteen stood exact, complete, unspent and spoken by nobody into Grain 8 (T061/T082).
Day 217 | Grain 8, Y.H. 813 (Eighthday) | under the Vail kiln-house roof at the bed, hearth, loaf shelf's low angle and kindling, the Clay Yard and its boards, the drying racks, the yard well, the bare trough, the flagstones, the wheel-shed edge, the finger's-width-open back room (not entered), the cold damper, the Debt Loft (heard only), the clay road, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | the boy came into his two hundred and seventeenth day and six months and thirty-seven days with nobody saying so, the carried fire's fifth unpaid for two hundred and seventeen days and the fire, river and boards keeping three counts that did not become one (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); the chapter's one meaningful change came on a wrong wind: a kiln-hand up from High Kiln with a barrow, lead glaze sweet at his mouth, let fall over the boards a third shaping of the old saying --- that there is a pot gone quiet on every row come the thaw --- so that three likenesses stood on the near-full tray with a finger between them, and the boy took them apart as a surgeon takes apart a wound and would not let them stand up together and call themselves a rule, for a rule is an account that has found a payer and no payer stood to any shaping, three likenesses not being three witnesses to one death and a silence reported not being a second hearing of the same pot, advanced and unpaid with no cause, no owner, no room, no figure, no lesson and no pot lost (T061/T082, T064); the sweet edge of lead glaze came up off High Kiln on that wrong wind, touched the fold and went out on the wind that brought it, a direction and not a second edge paid, no second green at a mouth and no seventh of anything (T064); Jorra, whose knowing of the full note had air under it and a want in it, kept it back from the wrong-wind man, let the saying stand as a saying, gave no name to what ailed the man carried out on Day 188, made no lesson of the green on the wind and lost no pot, his dusty forearms parted from his clean ones and his bent finger held in the cold damper until Sella's sentence reached the pot as a remembered repetition (T035/T031/T062);
