at the yard well a rack woman with slip dried pale in her wrists asked what the month's pots would want and a man off the clay road kept his number shut in his fist and put it back into his sleeve, both going to the pot and the weather with nothing priced, refused or entered, the printed one in twelve standing unchallenged, Berr's box locked up the road with the true subtraction answering nothing for the carried man, Hessa's slate unread at Greybank and the soft-cuffed clerk still set for his uncalled measure (T077, T015/T041/T047/T080/T090 held); Sella kept her back to the basket, her lips along the two-gapped row and her finger above the charcoal, set the pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand with a half-beat and no look toward the bed, and left the widow's mark untouched with no opening in thirty-seven days (T058/T079, T100); Tarn told the wood Six counted. The next I don't count and stopped the ear-sentence at the word that would call the loan, with the Seed 16 breath unspent, nine stoppings and no collector on Grain 8 (T036/T076); in the morning the hand went up an inch and came down before the counted breath had finished, an honest miss, and in the small hours, with no breathing number anywhere in the house, it kept the river's own low interval, the draw, the slack and the small pause, and came down on the next low note, the one-rhythm coordination remembered, filed on the sixth place and laid across nothing (T061/T082); two hundred and seventeen stood exact, unspent and spoken by nobody into Grain 9, the third shaping of the pot saying standing on its tray with no hand laying the three together and the wrong wind going out of the yard with the sweetness behind it and nothing paid.
Day 218 | Grain 9, Y.H. 813 (Ninthday) | under the Vail kiln-house roof at the bed, hearth, loaf shelf's low angle and kindling, the Clay Yard and its boards, the drying racks, the broken birth-slates beneath the lowest rack, the pan's gone round ring, the yard well, the bare trough, the flagstones, the wheel-shed edge, the finger's-width-open back room (not entered), the cold damper, the Debt Loft (heard only), the kiln mouth, the clay road toward the Cross, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | the boy came into his two hundred and eighteenth day and six months and thirty-eight days with nobody saying so, the carried fire's fifth unpaid for two hundred and eighteen days and fire, river and boards keeping three counts that did not become one (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); the burned-slate word came over the boards a fourth time from Slate Row, a voice along the boards where neighbours stood with their baskets, spoken over the boards and not to any basket on the same breath as the price of barley and the weather, with nobody saying whose slate, which room, what hand at the flame or why, and the boy took it apart as a surgeon takes apart a wound and set it on its own place beside Jorra's dust and the two shut-room tellings with a hand between the three --- four tellings of a slate and still not a third telling of the room shut in the war, a voice from a direction not a witness, four tellings not four witnesses to one fire, a direction arrived nowhere, not a cause, hearing, seventh, stopping or payment and nothing a clerk could chart, advanced and not paid; no slate burned under any roof and no fire was lit at the kiln mouth, for a fire would have cost something and the telling cost nothing, and he did not lay the word about a slate across the three sayings about a pot (T035/T031/T065/T095, T061/T082); Jorra grew quieter and the household straightened around the quiet without an order, the quiet going before him into the shed the way a cold air goes before a door, his claim from the Broken Slates gathering interest unpaid while he's not Vail stayed sealed and the promise of the boy's name neither given nor withdrawn, and he had once said a slate burns in four minutes and timed it but did not say it now; a neighbour's cost-question about the stalls going up for the tenth and a rack woman's at the well about the month's pots went to the pot and the weather and away level with nothing priced, refused or entered, while word went up the clay road of boards toward the Cross and of the reading block to be used on the morrow, the market not come and no slate opened, Berr's box locked with the true subtraction, Hessa's slate unread and the soft-cuffed clerk still set at the out-of-line landing (T077, T015/T041/T047/T080/T090 held); Sella kept her back to the basket and her finger above the charcoal, moved her lips along a two-gapped row without a figure, glanced once at the cold clay jar and shut her hand half over nothing, set the pot handle two inches beyond the reaching hand with a half-beat and no look toward the bed, pushed coal-black hair back with the burn-wrist so the grey streak showed pale, and left the widow's mark untouched with no opening in thirty-eight days, her private roll unshown and Dessa's cost unentered (T058/T079, T100); Garl was kept in objects with no lesson, no collector and his name unspoken for two hundred and eighteen days, the proverb gone to the pot and the tile debt circled without a name (T009/T013/T092); Tarn kept six where the house could check and the seventh at the lip beside Garl's unforged sentence, brought the ear-sentence to the word that would call the loan and stopped, filed it as a remembered stopping and not a new one, kept the Seed 16 breath unspent, nine stoppings with no tenth before the seventh year at its very end, the warm stone cold in its loft pot and no collector on Grain 9 (T036/T076, T006); in the morning the hand went up an inch and came down at once against the fold under conditions that had sometimes answered, an object on its own tray and no failure hidden under the miss (T061/T082); in the small hours, with Sella gone to her bed and no breathing number in the house, he took the hand through the intake, hold and letting go of one of the breaths she counts at her fire, kept it through the little silence after her count had ended and brought it down on the river's next low note --- the two-rhythm coordination remembered, not a change made again, filed on its sixth place with no payer named, and still not a longer reach, a miss, a hearing, a seventh, a stopping, a payment, a key, a power or a rule; he still could not close two inches to the pot handle, lift the crust or speak a number, and two hundred and eighteen went forward exact, complete, unspent and spoken by nobody into Day 219, Grain 10, with the seventh green owed as a colour and the seventh sighting of the foundling as a shape, neither come early and no loaf, slate or sale crossed the yard before the first market (T050/T051, T033/T034 held).
Day 219 | Grain 10, Y.H. 813 (Tenthday, the first Account-day and market of Grain) | the Vail kiln-house bed, hearth and loaf shelf's low angle, the Clay Yard and the clay road, the Cross and the moot-post's finger's width of shade, the reading block, the pot board and stalls, the weighing board, the out-of-line landing below the north bridge, the bed under the roof, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | the file turned out before the grey as instruments on a towel was exact, complete and worth nothing, six hearings beside and never among with the sixth from a person and no seventh, nine stoppings with no tenth before the seventh year at its very end, three held changes on three places with the loft scrape fourth, the pale line fifth and the held inch sixth, the burned-slate word told four times beside Jorra's dust and the two shut-room tellings with a hand between the three, and the pan's gone ring, the shelf's two gaps, the fold's crust and the field's empty place on trays that did not touch (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082, T036/T076, T035/T031/T065/T095); the boy was two hundred and nineteen days old and six months and thirty-nine days with nobody saying so, the fire's fifth unpaid for two hundred and nineteen days while the river's fifth note, the kiln's four-and-a-gap and the boards' four, four and a knot kept three counts in three places that did not become one, and the morning hand went up an inch and came down at once against the fold, a miss with no failure hidden under it filed on its sixth place (T061/T082); Sella Vail came to the fire before the basket, gave every instruction to the wool, fire, pot, broom, running water and river wind and never the basket, said the house, kept her finger above the charcoal and set the pot handle two inches beyond the hand with a half-beat and no look toward the bed, and carried him up at the half-pace from every open slate to set the basket in the moot-post's thin shade where no slate lay (T058/T079, T100); **the chapter's one meaningful change came as two sightings and no more:** across the square at the pot board a silent woman with clay in the small crescents under her nails, a pale line across the back of one hand and the sweet green of lead glaze beside her lip turned a cup a quarter without lifting it and set it back exactly where it stood, and the boy filed the seventh green as a colour on a place of its own beside six behind it, joined to no ear and never Fenna in the narration (T050/T051 paid as a sighting only); at the market's edge a shape in cast-offs with a man's shoulders, breeches cut off at the knee, silt dried pale in the wrist creases, a gap where a front tooth had been and a green sliver on a string that caught the light and went dull turned its head toward a set-down pot a half-beat late and stopped in the middle, and the boy filed the seventh sighting as a shape beside six behind it, never joined to his own turning, never Ulda in the narration, with no loaf, slate or sale and the sale to the Waiting Room clerk left years ahead (T033/T034 paid as a sighting only); he took the colour and the shape apart as a surgeon takes apart a wound and set them on places that do not touch --- a colour is not a hearing and a shape is not a seventh of the full note, neither a tenth stopping, cause, name or romance, and a fourth likeness does not stand up and call itself a rule because a rule is an account that has found a payer --- while Berr Clay came up short off the clay road with his office cord folded square under his coat, wiped his thumb twice before touching nothing, passed a finger over the figure and read one in twelve twice over the basket, said The levy is called. It is not called by me and your house, never I, and went off toward High Kiln with the box locked and the true subtraction inside believing he had done a kindness, Hessa Rale carried the slate up the road before noon with the writing to her sleeve and no figure coming off it, the bell went four, four and a gap with nobody crossing himself, eight pigs went over the weighing board and the soft-cuffed clerk stayed set at the out-of-line landing for a measure nobody called, so that the district's arithmetic changed where two hundred people stood and could not be changed back while the printed figure stood unchallenged and the figure read was still not the figure paid, answering nothing for the man carried out on Day 188 (T015/T041/T047, T077, T078, T080, T090, T058/T079); Sella carried him home at the going of the light at the half-pace, woodsmoke before in long low bars and river wind behind with water over water, wide around the pan's gone ring, the bare dark trough, the broken birth-slates, the field's empty place and the carriers' place with no number, set him on the bed and not the loft and left the thread frozen, and in the small hours the hand stayed for the small intake and came down before the letting go had finished --- an honest fraction shorter than the whole breath, filed on the sixth place and not a reach, hearing, seventh, stopping, payment, held change, key, power or rule (T098, T061/T082).
Day 220 | Grain 11, Y.H. 813 (Firstday) | under the Vail kiln-house roof at the bed, hearth, loaf shelf's low angle and kindling, the Clay Yard, the drying racks, the yard well and the bare trough, the flagstones, the Vail wheel-shed edge and its finger's-width-open back room (not entered), the cold damper, the Debt Loft (heard only), the clay road, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | an ordinary tending morning came after the market with no boundary drawn, the boy two hundred and twenty days old and six months and forty days with nobody saying so, the fire's fifth unpaid for two hundred and twenty days while fire, river and boards kept three counts in three places that did not become one, the reading block standing empty a day behind at the Cross (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); Grain's changed arithmetic lay outside the roof like weather and went unread under it --- printed one in twelve unchallenged, figure read not figure paid, true subtraction locked in Berr's box, Hessa's slate unread, soft-cuffed clerk still set --- and the seventh green and seventh shape stayed a colour and a shape a day old on places that do not touch, with no eighth, no second look joined, no name and no promotion into a hearing, cause or romance (T015/T041/T047, T077, T080, T090 held, T050/T051, T033/T034 held advanced); cost-questions at the door and the well went to the pot and the weather and away level, a man off the clay road put his fist's number back into his sleeve so it arrived nowhere, and the yard went around the clean dark line, the finer grey called thaw, the broken birth-slates, the pan's gone round ring, the bare dark trough and the field's empty place with a look and a wide step and no number, no third gap coming and no fourth likeness made a cause of two absences (T077, T009/T013/T092/T098); Sella came to the fire before the basket, said the house, moved her lips along a two-gapped row without a figure, glanced once at the cold clay jar and shut her hand half over nothing, kept her finger on top of the shelf's front edge so the charcoal stayed unread, set the pot handle two inches beyond the hand with the half-beat and no look toward the bed, and left the widow's mark untouched with no opening in forty days and Dessa's cost unentered (T058/T079, T100); Tarn took the broom only after it was instructed, told the wood Six counted. The next I don't count with six in the worn thumb-mark and the seventh at the lip beside Garl's unforged collector sentence, stopped the ear-sentence at the word that would call the loan and filed the stopping as a remembered one, kept the Seed 16 breath unspent and nine stoppings with no tenth before the seventh year at its very end, swept past the bright patch without the half-check and over the collector's road without a turn, and no collector came on Grain 11 while the stone stayed cold in its loft pot (T036/T076, T006, T062); Jorra came through with meal-fine slate dust white in the creases of a fist, kept dusty and clean forearms on one tray, spoke to the wheel and told a pot You'll do, withheld the knowing that had air under it and a want, let the three sayings stand as sayings, gave no name to what ailed the man carried out on Day 188, held his bent finger in the cold damper until Sella's sentence reached the pot as a remembered repetition, kept he's not Vail sealed and his promise ungiven, and grew quieter under the burned-slate word told four times while three or more unnamed men went low into the back room before first grey and got no name (T035/T031/T062/T065/T095, T061/T082); three or more men went low into the back room before first grey and no third telling of the counting room came (T065/T095); the morning hand went up an inch and came down before the counted breath had finished, down with the hold still ahead of it, an honest miss filed on its sixth place and laid across nothing, and in the small hours, with no count anywhere in the house, it kept the river's own low interval --- draw, slack, small pause --- and came down on the next low note, the one-rhythm coordination remembered and not a change made again (T061/T082).
Day 221 | Grain 12, Y.H. 813 (Secondday) | under the Vail kiln-house roof at the bed, hearth, loaf shelf's low angle and kindling, the Clay Yard, the drying racks, the yard well and the bare trough, the flagstones, the Vail wheel-shed edge and its finger's-width-open back room (not entered), the cold damper, the Debt Loft (heard only), the clay road, Low Kiln, Sill, Grath | the boy came into his two hundred and twenty-first day and six months and forty-one days with nobody saying so, the fire's fifth unpaid for two hundred and twenty-one days while fire, river and boards kept three counts in three places that did not become one, Grain 10 two mornings behind and Grain 20 (Day 229) eight mornings ahead with neither brought early (T071/T072, T088, T061/T082); the district's changed arithmetic went on unread under the roof and the seventh green and seventh shape stayed a colour and a shape two days gathered, with no eighth, no joining, no name, no loaf, no slate and no sale (T050/T051, T033/T034 held advanced); a wrong wind came up off High Kiln before first grey with the sweet edge of lead glaze and a voice over the boards that let one of the three shapings of the pot saying stand --- a pot gone quiet on every row come the thaw --- and went on to the price of barley and the weather, and the boy took it apart as a surgeon takes apart a wound and left it a saying about a pot with no pot lost, no fourth likeness made a cause and no name for what ailed the man carried out on Day 188 (T061/T082, T064); cost-questions went to the pot and the weather and arrived nowhere, and the yard went around the clean dark line, the new finer grey called thaw, the rack space with its broken birth-slates, the pan's gone round ring, the bare dark trough and the field's empty place with a look and a wide step and no number (T077, T009/T013/T092/T098); Sella kept her instruction grammar, her half-pace, her jar-glance and hand half-shut, her lips along a two-gapped row with no figure, her finger above the charcoal, and set the pot handle two inches beyond the hand with the half-beat at the setting and no look toward the bed, the widow's mark untouched with no opening in forty-one days and her private roll unshown (T058/T079, T100); Tarn said Six counted. The next I don't count with six where the house can check and the seventh at the lip, stopped the ear-sentence at the calling word as a remembered stopping, kept nine with no tenth before the seventh year at its very end, the Seed 16 breath unspent and the loan uncalled, and no collector on Grain 12, the warm stone cold in its loft pot (T036/T076, T006, T062); Jorra kept dusty and clean forearms on one tray, told a pot You'll do and withheld the note, held his bent finger in the damper until Sella's sentence reached the pot as a remembered repetition, kept the burned-slate word at four tellings and the two shut-room tellings at two, sealed he's not Vail and left his promise ungiven, while three or more unnamed men went low into the back room before first grey and got no name (T035/T031/T062/T065/T095); the morning hand went up an inch toward the note and came down at once against the fold under conditions that had sometimes answered --- a miss with no failure hidden under it, filed on the sixth place --- and in the small hours he turned out the file as instruments on a towel and found it exact, complete and worth nothing: six hearings beside and never among with no seventh, three near-full answers, three shapings of the pot saying, told bell beside heard and told figure beside paid, nine stoppings with no tenth, three held changes with the loft scrape fourth, pale line fifth and held inch sixth, the seventh green a colour and the seventh shape a shape on places that do not touch, and the burned-slate word beside the dust and the two shut-room tellings with a hand between the three; then, with Sella gone to her bed and the two rooms not touching, the hand went up an inch, was taken through the intake, hold and letting go of one of Sella's counted breaths, kept through the little silence after her count had ended and came down on the river's next low note --- the two-rhythm coordination remembered, not a longer reach, a miss, a hearing, a seventh, a stopping, a payment, a held change, a key, a power or a rule, with no payer named and nothing joined, named, opened, called, spent or ended (T061/T082, T050/T051, T033/T034, T036/T076, T035/T031); two hundred and twenty-one stood exact, complete, unspent and spoken by nobody in Sarn, Sill or Grath, the seventh year at its very end and still ungiven, the charcoal unread and the stone cold in its loft pot, and the count went forward into Day 222, Grain 13, with nothing settled and nothing ended.
